        /* =========================================================================
           THEME SWITCHER (DARK SKY / LIGHT SKY) & AUTH SLIDER
           ========================================================================= */
        window.toggleAppColorTheme = function() {
            const htmlEl = document.documentElement;
            const current = htmlEl.getAttribute("data-theme") || "dark";
            const next = current === "dark" ? "light" : "dark";
            htmlEl.setAttribute("data-theme", next);
            localStorage.setItem("vestify_theme", next);
            updateThemeIconUI(next);
            triggerToastDisplay(next === "light" ? "Sky Light Mode Activated" : "Sky Dark Mode Activated");
        };


        function updateThemeIconUI(theme) {
            const icon = document.getElementById("themeToggleIcon");
            if (icon) {
                icon.className = theme === "light" ? "fa-solid fa-moon" : "fa-solid fa-sun";
            }
        }


        function startAuthBackgroundSlider() {
            const slides = document.querySelectorAll(".auth-slide");
            if (!slides.length) return;
            let idx = 0;
            setInterval(() => {
                slides[idx].classList.remove("active");
                idx = (idx + 1) % slides.length;
                slides[idx].classList.add("active");
            }, 4500);
        }


        function getTotalPlayableBalance() {
            return userWithdrawableBalance + userBonusBalance;
        }


        /* =========================================================================
           CENTRALIZED GAME WALLET HELPER (SHARED BY AVIATOR, DT, AB, MINES)
           ========================================================================= */
        function placeSharedGameBet(amount) {
            if (!currentUserId) {
                triggerToastDisplay("Please sign in to play.");
                return false;
            }
            if (isNaN(amount) || amount < 10) {
                triggerToastDisplay("Minimum bet is Rs. 10.");
                return false;
            }
            if (amount > getTotalPlayableBalance()) {
                triggerToastDisplay("Insufficient balance! Please deposit funds.");
                return false;
            }
            deductPlayableBalance(amount);
            return true;
        }


        function creditSharedGameWin(payoutAmount) {
            if (!currentUserId || payoutAmount <= 0) return;
            userWithdrawableBalance += payoutAmount;
            updateWalletUI();
            db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId).update({
                withdrawableBalance: userWithdrawableBalance,
                balance: getTotalPlayableBalance()
            });
        }


        function deductPlayableBalance(amount) {
            let fromBonus = Math.min(userBonusBalance, amount);
            let fromWithdrawable = amount - fromBonus;
            userBonusBalance -= fromBonus;
            userWithdrawableBalance -= fromWithdrawable;


            aviatorWagerProgress += amount;
            let newSpinsEarned = Math.floor(aviatorWagerProgress / 5000);
            if (newSpinsEarned > 0) {
                aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable += newSpinsEarned;
                aviatorWagerProgress = aviatorWagerProgress % 5000;
                triggerToastDisplay(`Unlocked ${newSpinsEarned} VIP Free Spin! Claim in Aviator.`);
            }


            updateWalletUI();
            if (currentUserId) {
                db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId).update({
                    bonusBalance: userBonusBalance,
                    withdrawableBalance: userWithdrawableBalance,
                    balance: getTotalPlayableBalance(),
                    aviatorWagerProgress: aviatorWagerProgress,
                    aviatorFreeSpins: aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable
                });
            }
        }


        /* =========================================================================
           CASINO GAMES HUB (DRAGON TIGER, ANDAR BAHAR, MINES)
           ========================================================================= */
        window.switchCasinoHubGame = function(gameKey) {
            const ge = systemSettings.gamesEnabled || {};
            if (gameKey === "dt" && ge.dragonTiger === false) { triggerToastDisplay("Dragon Tiger is paused by Admin."); return; }
            if (gameKey === "ab" && ge.andarBahar === false) { triggerToastDisplay("Andar Bahar is paused by Admin."); return; }
            if (gameKey === "mines" && ge.mines === false) { triggerToastDisplay("Mines is paused by Admin."); return; }
            const map = {
                dt: ["hubTabDT", "gamePaneDT"],
                ab: ["hubTabAB", "gamePaneAB"],
                mines: ["hubTabMines", "gamePaneMines"]
            };
            Object.keys(map).forEach(k => {
                document.getElementById(map[k][0]).classList.toggle("active", k === gameKey);
                document.getElementById(map[k][1]).classList.toggle("active", k === gameKey);
            });
        };


        const cardSuits = [
            { symbol: "♥", color: "card-red" },
            { symbol: "♦", color: "card-red" },
            { symbol: "♠", color: "card-black" },
            { symbol: "♣", color: "card-black" }
        ];
        const cardRanks = [
            { label: "A", val: 1 }, { label: "2", val: 2 }, { label: "3", val: 3 }, { label: "4", val: 4 },
            { label: "5", val: 5 }, { label: "6", val: 6 }, { label: "7", val: 7 }, { label: "8", val: 8 },
            { label: "9", val: 9 }, { label: "10", val: 10 }, { label: "J", val: 11 }, { label: "Q", val: 12 }, { label: "K", val: 13 }
        ];


        function drawRandomPlayingCard() {
            const suit = cardSuits[Math.floor(Math.random() * cardSuits.length)];
            const rank = cardRanks[Math.floor(Math.random() * cardRanks.length)];
            return { label: rank.label, val: rank.val, suit: suit.symbol, color: suit.color };
        }


        /* --- 1. DRAGON TIGER ENGINE --- */
        const dtState = {
            selectedChip: 10,
            phase: "BETTING",
            timeLeft: 15,
            bets: { dragon: 0, tie: 0, tiger: 0 },
            lastBets: { dragon: 0, tie: 0, tiger: 0 },
            history: ["D", "T", "D", "D", "TIE", "T", "D", "T"]
        };


        window.selectDtChip = function(val, btn) {
            dtState.selectedChip = val;
            document.querySelectorAll("#dtChipsContainer .poker-chip").forEach(c => c.classList.remove("selected"));
            if (btn) btn.classList.add("selected");
        };


        window.placeDtChipBet = function(zone) {
            if (dtState.phase !== "BETTING") {
                triggerToastDisplay("Wait for next Dragon Tiger round.");
                return;
            }
            if (!placeSharedGameBet(dtState.selectedChip)) return;
            dtState.bets[zone] += dtState.selectedChip;
            playAviatorTone("bet");
            updateDtBetLabels();
        };


        window.clearDtCurrentBets = function() {
            if (dtState.phase !== "BETTING") return;
            const total = dtState.bets.dragon + dtState.bets.tie + dtState.bets.tiger;
            if (total > 0) {
                creditSharedGameWin(total);
                dtState.bets = { dragon: 0, tie: 0, tiger: 0 };
                updateDtBetLabels();
                triggerToastDisplay("Dragon Tiger bets cleared & refunded.");
            }
        };


        window.repeatDtLastBets = function() {
            if (dtState.phase !== "BETTING") return;
            const total = dtState.lastBets.dragon + dtState.lastBets.tie + dtState.lastBets.tiger;
            if (total <= 0) { triggerToastDisplay("No previous bet to repeat."); return; }
            if (!placeSharedGameBet(total)) return;
            dtState.bets.dragon += dtState.lastBets.dragon;
            dtState.bets.tie += dtState.lastBets.tie;
            dtState.bets.tiger += dtState.lastBets.tiger;
            playAviatorTone("bet");
            updateDtBetLabels();
        };


        function updateDtBetLabels() {
            document.getElementById("dtBetLblDragon").innerText = `Rs. ${dtState.bets.dragon}`;
            document.getElementById("dtBetLblTie").innerText = `Rs. ${dtState.bets.tie}`;
            document.getElementById("dtBetLblTiger").innerText = `Rs. ${dtState.bets.tiger}`;
        }


        function renderDtTrends() {
            const el = document.getElementById("dtTrendStrip");
            if (!el) return;
            el.innerHTML = dtState.history.map(item => {
                const bg = item === "D" ? "#ef4444" : (item === "T" ? "#eab308" : "#10b981");
                const txt = item === "T" ? "#0f172a" : "#fff";
                return `<span style="background:${bg}; color:${txt}; font-size:10px; font-weight:800; padding:2px 7px; border-radius:99px; flex-shrink:0;">${item}</span>`;
            }).join("");
        }


        
        function renderCasinoTableLiveBets(feedId, labelsList, activeSide = null, activeStake = 0) {
            const el = document.getElementById(feedId);
            if (!el) return;
            const stakes = [50, 100, 150, 250, 500, 1000, 2000, 3500, 5000];
            const rowCount = Math.floor(5 + Math.random() * 6);
            const rows = [];


            if (activeSide && activeStake > 0) {
                const youLabel = (typeof userCustomUsernameToken !== "undefined" && userCustomUsernameToken)
                    ? userCustomUsernameToken
                    : "YOU";
                rows.push(`<div class="live-table-bet-row" style="border:1px solid rgba(56,189,248,0.4); background:rgba(14,165,233,0.14);"><span><b>${youLabel}</b> <span class="live-badge-real">LIVE</span> <span style="color:#38bdf8;">[${activeSide}]</span></span><b style="color:#10b981;">Rs. ${Number(activeStake).toLocaleString()}</b></div>`);
            }


            for (let i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
                const p = (typeof generateRandomMaskedPlayerIdentity === "function")
                    ? generateRandomMaskedPlayerIdentity()
                    : `03${Math.floor(10 + Math.random() * 39)}****${Math.floor(10 + Math.random() * 89)}`;
                const side = labelsList[Math.floor(Math.random() * labelsList.length)];
                const amt = stakes[Math.floor(Math.random() * stakes.length)];
                rows.push(`<div class="live-table-bet-row"><span><b>${p}</b> <span style="color:#38bdf8;">[${side}]</span></span><b style="color:#10b981;">Rs. ${amt.toLocaleString()}</b></div>`);
            }
            el.innerHTML = rows.join("");
        }


        function startDragonTigerLoop() {
            renderCasinoTableLiveBets('dtLiveBetsFeed', ['DRAGON', 'TIGER', 'TIE']);
            renderDtTrends();
            setInterval(() => {
                const badge = document.getElementById("dtTimerBadge");
                const statusEl = document.getElementById("dtStatusBanner");
                if (!badge) return;


                if (dtState.phase === "BETTING") {
                    dtState.timeLeft--;
                    badge.innerText = `Betting: ${dtState.timeLeft}s`;
                    badge.style.background = "#eab308";


                    if (dtState.timeLeft <= 0) {
                        dtState.phase = "REVEAL";
                        badge.innerText = "Dealing...";
                        badge.style.background = "#ef4444";
                        statusEl.innerText = "No more bets! Revealing cards...";


                        const dCard = drawRandomPlayingCard();
                        const tCard = drawRandomPlayingCard();


                        setTimeout(() => {
                            const dSlot = document.getElementById("dtDragonCardSlot");
                            const tSlot = document.getElementById("dtTigerCardSlot");
                            dSlot.classList.add("revealed");
                            tSlot.classList.add("revealed");
                            dSlot.innerHTML = `<span class="${dCard.color}" style="font-size:22px;">${dCard.label}</span><span class="${dCard.color}" style="font-size:20px;">${dCard.suit}</span>`;
                            tSlot.innerHTML = `<span class="${tCard.color}" style="font-size:22px;">${tCard.label}</span><span class="${tCard.color}" style="font-size:20px;">${tCard.suit}</span>`;


                            let winner = "tie";
                            if (dCard.val > tCard.val) winner = "dragon";
                            else if (tCard.val > dCard.val) winner = "tiger";
                            if (systemSettings.forceDtWinner && systemSettings.forceDtWinner !== "AUTO") {
                                winner = systemSettings.forceDtWinner;
                                systemSettings.forceDtWinner = "AUTO";
                            }


                            document.getElementById("dtZoneDragon").classList.toggle("winner-glow", winner === "dragon");
                            document.getElementById("dtZoneTie").classList.toggle("winner-glow", winner === "tie");
                            document.getElementById("dtZoneTiger").classList.toggle("winner-glow", winner === "tiger");


                            let payout = 0;
                            if (winner === "dragon" && dtState.bets.dragon > 0) payout += dtState.bets.dragon * 2;
                            if (winner === "tiger" && dtState.bets.tiger > 0) payout += dtState.bets.tiger * 2;
                            if (winner === "tie" && dtState.bets.tie > 0) payout += dtState.bets.tie * 9;
                            if (winner === "tie" && (dtState.bets.dragon > 0 || dtState.bets.tiger > 0)) {
                                payout += (dtState.bets.dragon + dtState.bets.tiger) * 0.5;
                            }


                            if (payout > 0) {
                                creditSharedGameWin(payout);
                                playAviatorTone("cashout");
                                triggerToastDisplay(`Dragon Tiger (${winner.toUpperCase()}) Won +Rs. ${payout.toFixed(0)}!`);
                            }


                            statusEl.innerText = `${winner.toUpperCase()} WINS! (${dCard.label}${dCard.suit} vs ${tCard.label}${tCard.suit})`;
                            dtState.history.unshift(winner === "dragon" ? "D" : (winner === "tiger" ? "T" : "TIE"));
                            if (dtState.history.length > 16) dtState.history.pop();
                            renderDtTrends();


                            setTimeout(() => {
                                if (dtState.bets.dragon + dtState.bets.tie + dtState.bets.tiger > 0) {
                                    dtState.lastBets = { ...dtState.bets };
                                }
                                dtState.bets = { dragon: 0, tie: 0, tiger: 0 };
                                updateDtBetLabels();
                                dSlot.classList.remove("revealed");
                                tSlot.classList.remove("revealed");
                                dSlot.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-dragon" style="font-size:24px; color:#ef4444; opacity:0.6;"></i>`;
                                tSlot.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-paw" style="font-size:24px; color:#eab308; opacity:0.6;"></i>`;
                                document.querySelectorAll("#gamePaneDT .table-bet-zone").forEach(z => z.classList.remove("winner-glow"));
                                dtState.phase = "BETTING";
                                dtState.timeLeft = 15;
                                statusEl.innerText = "Select a chip & tap Dragon, Tie, or Tiger";
                            }, 3500);
                        }, 700);
                    }
                }
            }, 1000);
        }


        /* --- 2. ANDAR BAHAR ENGINE --- */
        const abState = {
            selectedChip: 10,
            phase: "BETTING",
            timeLeft: 15,
            bets: { andar: 0, bahar: 0 },
            lastBets: { andar: 0, bahar: 0 },
            history: ["A", "B", "A", "A", "B", "B", "A"]
        };


        window.selectAbChip = function(val, btn) {
            abState.selectedChip = val;
            document.querySelectorAll("#gamePaneAB .poker-chip").forEach(c => c.classList.remove("selected"));
            if (btn) btn.classList.add("selected");
        };


        window.placeAbChipBet = function(side) {
            if (abState.phase !== "BETTING") {
                triggerToastDisplay("Wait for next Andar Bahar round.");
                return;
            }
            if (!placeSharedGameBet(abState.selectedChip)) return;
            abState.bets[side] += abState.selectedChip;
            playAviatorTone("bet");
            updateAbBetLabels();
        };


        window.clearAbCurrentBets = function() {
            if (abState.phase !== "BETTING") return;
            const total = abState.bets.andar + abState.bets.bahar;
            if (total > 0) {
                creditSharedGameWin(total);
                abState.bets = { andar: 0, bahar: 0 };
                updateAbBetLabels();
                triggerToastDisplay("Andar Bahar bets cleared & refunded.");
            }
        };


        window.repeatAbLastBets = function() {
            if (abState.phase !== "BETTING") return;
            const total = abState.lastBets.andar + abState.lastBets.bahar;
            if (total <= 0) { triggerToastDisplay("No previous bet to repeat."); return; }
            if (!placeSharedGameBet(total)) return;
            abState.bets.andar += abState.lastBets.andar;
            abState.bets.bahar += abState.lastBets.bahar;
            playAviatorTone("bet");
            updateAbBetLabels();
        };


        function updateAbBetLabels() {
            document.getElementById("abBetLblAndar").innerText = `Rs. ${abState.bets.andar}`;
            document.getElementById("abBetLblBahar").innerText = `Rs. ${abState.bets.bahar}`;
        }


        function renderAbTrends() {
            const el = document.getElementById("abTrendStrip");
            if (!el) return;
            el.innerHTML = abState.history.map(item => {
                const bg = item === "A" ? "#0ea5e9" : "#ef4444";
                return `<span style="background:${bg}; color:#fff; font-size:10px; font-weight:800; padding:2px 8px; border-radius:99px; flex-shrink:0;">${item}</span>`;
            }).join("");
        }


        
        function startAndarBaharLoop() {
            renderCasinoTableLiveBets('abLiveBetsFeed', ['ANDAR', 'BAHAR']);
            renderAbTrends();
            setInterval(() => {
                const badge = document.getElementById("abTimerBadge");
                const statusEl = document.getElementById("abStatusBanner");
                if (!badge) return;


                if (abState.phase === "BETTING") {
                    abState.timeLeft--;
                    badge.innerText = `Betting: ${abState.timeLeft}s`;
                    badge.style.background = "#eab308";


                    if (abState.timeLeft <= 0) {
                        abState.phase = "DEALING";
                        badge.innerText = "Dealing...";
                        badge.style.background = "#ef4444";


                        const joker = drawRandomPlayingCard();
                        const jSlot = document.getElementById("abJokerCardSlot");
                        jSlot.classList.add("revealed");
                        jSlot.innerHTML = `<span class="${joker.color}" style="font-size:20px;">${joker.label}</span><span class="${joker.color}" style="font-size:18px;">${joker.suit}</span>`;
                        statusEl.innerText = `Joker is ${joker.label}${joker.suit} — Dealing Andar & Bahar...`;


                        const andarTrack = document.getElementById("abAndarTrack");
                        const baharTrack = document.getElementById("abBaharTrack");
                        andarTrack.innerHTML = "";
                        baharTrack.innerHTML = "";


                        let step = 0;
                        let matchedSide = null;
                        const dealTimer = setInterval(() => {
                            const side = step % 2 === 0 ? "andar" : "bahar";
                            let card = drawRandomPlayingCard();
                            if (step >= 10 && card.label !== joker.label) {
                                card = { ...joker, suit: cardSuits[step % 4].symbol, color: cardSuits[step % 4].color };
                            }


                            const cardHtml = `<div class="mini-deal-card ${card.color}"><span>${card.label}</span><span>${card.suit}</span></div>`;
                            if (side === "andar") {
                                andarTrack.innerHTML += cardHtml;
                                andarTrack.scrollLeft = andarTrack.scrollWidth;
                            } else {
                                baharTrack.innerHTML += cardHtml;
                                baharTrack.scrollLeft = baharTrack.scrollWidth;
                            }


                            if (card.label === joker.label) {
                                clearInterval(dealTimer);
                                matchedSide = side;
                                if (systemSettings.forceAbWinner && systemSettings.forceAbWinner !== "AUTO") {
                                    matchedSide = systemSettings.forceAbWinner;
                                    systemSettings.forceAbWinner = "AUTO";
                                }


                                document.getElementById("abZoneAndar").classList.toggle("winner-glow", matchedSide === "andar");
                                document.getElementById("abZoneBahar").classList.toggle("winner-glow", matchedSide === "bahar");


                                let payout = 0;
                                if (matchedSide === "andar" && abState.bets.andar > 0) payout = abState.bets.andar * 1.9;
                                if (matchedSide === "bahar" && abState.bets.bahar > 0) payout = abState.bets.bahar * 2.0;


                                if (payout > 0) {
                                    creditSharedGameWin(payout);
                                    playAviatorTone("cashout");
                                    triggerToastDisplay(`Andar Bahar (${matchedSide.toUpperCase()}) Won +Rs. ${payout.toFixed(0)}!`);
                                }


                                statusEl.innerText = `MATCHED ON ${matchedSide.toUpperCase()}!`;
                                abState.history.unshift(matchedSide === "andar" ? "A" : "B");
                                if (abState.history.length > 16) abState.history.pop();
                                renderAbTrends();


                                setTimeout(() => {
                                    if (abState.bets.andar + abState.bets.bahar > 0) {
                                        abState.lastBets = { ...abState.bets };
                                    }
                                    abState.bets = { andar: 0, bahar: 0 };
                                    updateAbBetLabels();
                                    jSlot.classList.remove("revealed");
                                    jSlot.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-crown" style="font-size:20px; color:#eab308;"></i>`;
                                    andarTrack.innerHTML = "";
                                    baharTrack.innerHTML = "";
                                    document.querySelectorAll("#gamePaneAB .table-bet-zone").forEach(z => z.classList.remove("winner-glow"));
                                    abState.phase = "BETTING";
                                    abState.timeLeft = 15;
                                    statusEl.innerText = "Place your bet on Andar or Bahar";
                                }, 3200);
                            }
                            step++;
                        }, 420);
                    }
                }
            }, 1000);
        }


        /* --- 3. MINES (5x5 GRID) ENGINE --- */
        const minesState = {
            active: false,
            betAmount: 50,
            minesCount: 4,
            minePositions: new Set(),
            revealedPositions: new Set(),
            currentMultiplier: 1.00
        };


        function getSafeMinesCount(rawVal) {
            const minAllowed = Math.max(4, parseInt(systemSettings.minMinesAllowed ?? 4, 10) || 4);
            const parsed = parseInt(rawVal, 10);
            if (isNaN(parsed)) return minAllowed;
            return Math.max(minAllowed, Math.min(24, parsed));
        }


        function calculateMinesStepMultiplier(mines, safeRevealedCount) {
            const safeMines = getSafeMinesCount(mines);
            const edgePct = Math.max(4, Math.min(45, Number(systemSettings.minesHouseEdgePct ?? 8)));
            let mult = (100 - edgePct) / 100;
            for (let i = 0; i < safeRevealedCount; i++) {
                mult *= (25 - i) / (25 - safeMines - i);
            }
            return Math.max(1.01, Number(mult.toFixed(2)));
        }


        function renderMinesGrid() {
            const container = document.getElementById("minesGridContainer");
            if (!container) return;
            let html = "";
            for (let i = 0; i < 25; i++) {
                const isRevealed = minesState.revealedPositions.has(i);
                const isMine = minesState.minePositions.has(i);
                let cls = "mine-tile";
                let inner = `<i class="fa-solid fa-question" style="font-size:13px; opacity:0.35;"></i>`;


                if (isRevealed) {
                    if (isMine) {
                        cls += " bomb-revealed";
                        inner = `<i class="fa-solid fa-bomb"></i>`;
                    } else {
                        cls += " safe-revealed";
                        inner = `<i class="fa-solid fa-gem"></i>`;
                    }
                } else if (!minesState.active && minesState.minePositions.size > 0) {
                    cls += " dim-revealed";
                    inner = isMine ? `<i class="fa-solid fa-bomb" style="color:#ef4444;"></i>` : `<i class="fa-solid fa-gem" style="color:#10b981;"></i>`;
                }


                html += `<div class="${cls}" onclick="clickMineTile(${i})">${inner}</div>`;
            }
            container.innerHTML = html;
        }


        window.updateMinesPreviewMultiplier = function() {
            const mines = getSafeMinesCount(document.getElementById("minesCountSelect").value);
            const nextMult = calculateMinesStepMultiplier(mines, minesState.revealedPositions.size + 1);
            document.getElementById("minesNextMultLbl").innerText = `${nextMult.toFixed(2)}x`;
        };


        window.adjustMinesBet = function(action) {
            if (minesState.active) return;
            const inp = document.getElementById("minesBetInput");
            let val = parseFloat(inp.value) || 50;
            if (action === "min") val = 10;
            else if (action === "half") val = Math.max(10, Math.floor(val / 2));
            else if (action === "double") val = Math.min(50000, val * 2);
            else if (action === "max") val = Math.max(10, Math.floor(Math.min(50000, getTotalPlayableBalance())));
            inp.value = val;
        };


        window.handleMinesMainAction = function() {
            const btn = document.getElementById("btnMinesMainAction");
            const randBtn = document.getElementById("btnMinesRandomTile");


            if (!minesState.active) {
                const bet = parseFloat(document.getElementById("minesBetInput").value);
                const mines = getSafeMinesCount(document.getElementById("minesCountSelect").value);
                if (isNaN(bet) || bet < 10 || bet > 50000) {
                    triggerToastDisplay("Bet must be between Rs. 10 and Rs. 50,000.");
                    return;
                }
                if (!placeSharedGameBet(bet)) return;


                minesState.active = true;
                minesState.betAmount = bet;
                minesState.minesCount = mines;
                minesState.revealedPositions.clear();
                minesState.minePositions.clear();
                minesState.currentMultiplier = 1.00;


                while (minesState.minePositions.size < mines) {
                    minesState.minePositions.add(Math.floor(Math.random() * 25));
                }


                playAviatorTone("bet");
                document.getElementById("minesCurrentMultLbl").innerText = "1.00x";
                updateMinesPreviewMultiplier();
                btn.innerText = "PICK A TILE TO WIN";
                btn.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg, #10b981, #059669)";
                randBtn.style.display = "inline-block";
                renderMinesGrid();
            } else {
                if (minesState.revealedPositions.size === 0) {
                    triggerToastDisplay("Open at least 1 safe tile before cashing out!");
                    return;
                }
                const payout = minesState.betAmount * minesState.currentMultiplier;
                minesState.active = false;
                creditSharedGameWin(payout);
                playAviatorTone("cashout");
                triggerToastDisplay(`Mines Cashed Out @ ${minesState.currentMultiplier.toFixed(2)}x! +Rs. ${payout.toFixed(2)}`);


                btn.innerText = "START MINES GAME";
                btn.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg, #0ea5e9, #0284c7)";
                randBtn.style.display = "none";
                renderMinesGrid();
            }
        };


        window.clickMineTile = function(idx) {
            if (!minesState.active || minesState.revealedPositions.has(idx)) return;
            if (systemSettings.forceNextMineBomb) {
                minesState.minePositions.add(idx);
                systemSettings.forceNextMineBomb = false;
                const cb = document.getElementById("adminForceNextMineBomb");
                if (cb) cb.checked = false;
            }
            minesState.revealedPositions.add(idx);


            if (minesState.minePositions.has(idx)) {
                minesState.active = false;
                playAviatorTone("crash");
                triggerToastDisplay("BOOM! You hit a mine!");
                const btn = document.getElementById("btnMinesMainAction");
                btn.innerText = "START MINES GAME";
                btn.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg, #0ea5e9, #0284c7)";
                document.getElementById("btnMinesRandomTile").style.display = "none";
                renderMinesGrid();
                return;
            }


            minesState.currentMultiplier = calculateMinesStepMultiplier(minesState.minesCount, minesState.revealedPositions.size);
            const liveWin = minesState.betAmount * minesState.currentMultiplier;
            document.getElementById("minesCurrentMultLbl").innerText = `${minesState.currentMultiplier.toFixed(2)}x`;
            updateMinesPreviewMultiplier();


            const btn = document.getElementById("btnMinesMainAction");
            btn.innerText = `CASH OUT — Rs. ${liveWin.toFixed(2)}`;
            playAviatorTone("bet");
            renderMinesGrid();


            if (minesState.revealedPositions.size >= 25 - minesState.minesCount) {
                handleMinesMainAction();
            }
        };


        window.pickRandomMineTile = function() {
            if (!minesState.active) return;
            const unrevealed = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < 25; i++) {
                if (!minesState.revealedPositions.has(i)) unrevealed.push(i);
            }
            if (unrevealed.length > 0) {
                const pick = unrevealed[Math.floor(Math.random() * unrevealed.length)];
                clickMineTile(pick);
            }
        };

        const aviatorState = {
            roundId: "RND-" + Date.now(),
            status: "BETTING",
            bettingEndsAt: Date.now() + 10000,
            flightStartedAt: null,
            currentMultiplier: 1.00,
            crashPoint: 2.00,
            soundEnabled: true,
            audioCtx: null,
            sessionStartTime: Date.now(),
            displayedPlayerCount: 284,
            panels: {
                1: { activeBet: false, betAmount: 0, autoBetRemaining: 0 },
                2: { activeBet: false, betAmount: 0, autoBetRemaining: 0 }
            },
            recentHistory: [1.45, 2.35, 1.18, 14.80, 1.92, 4.10, 215.50, 1.32],
            personalBetsList: [],
            simulatedBots: [],
            realLiveFirestoreBets: [],
            renderRaf: null,
            loopInterval: null
        };


        const randomPakistaniNames = [
            "hamza_pk", "ali_khan", "zainab99", "usman_rwp", "bilal_pro", "sana_m", "tariq_gb", "ahmed_77",

        function generateRandomMaskedPlayerIdentity() {
            if (Math.random() < 0.5) {
                const prefixes = ["0300", "0301", "0312", "0315", "0321", "0333", "0334", "0345", "0346", "0318"];
                const pref = prefixes[Math.floor(Math.random() * prefixes.length)];
                const end = String(Math.floor(10 + Math.random() * 89));
                return `${pref}****${end}`;
            } else {
                const base = randomPakistaniNames[Math.floor(Math.random() * randomPakistaniNames.length)];
                const suffix = Math.random() < 0.6 ? Math.floor(10 + Math.random() * 899) : "";
                return `${base}${suffix}`;
            }
        }


        function generateMassiveSimulatedCrowd() {
            const count = Math.floor(65 + Math.random() * 45);
            const betPool = [50, 100, 150, 200, 300, 500, 750, 1000, 1500, 2000, 3500, 5000, 10000, 15000, 25000];
            const list = [];


            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const betAmt = betPool[Math.floor(Math.random() * betPool.length)];
                const r = Math.random();
                let target = 1.5;
                if (r < 0.45) target = parseFloat((1.15 + Math.random() * 0.85).toFixed(2));
                else if (r < 0.80) target = parseFloat((2.00 + Math.random() * 3.50).toFixed(2));
                else if (r < 0.95) target = parseFloat((5.50 + Math.random() * 14.50).toFixed(2));
                else target = parseFloat((20.00 + Math.random() * 120.00).toFixed(2));


                list.push({
                    name: generateRandomMaskedPlayerIdentity(),
                    bet: betAmt,
                    targetMult: target
                });
            }
            aviatorState.simulatedBots = list;
            updateDynamicPlayerCounter(true);
        }


        function updateDynamicPlayerCounter(isNewRound = false) {
            const base = systemSettings.crowdBaseCount || 280;
            const realCount = aviatorState.realLiveFirestoreBets.length;
            if (isNewRound) {
                aviatorState.displayedPlayerCount = base + Math.floor(Math.random() * 160) + realCount;
            } else {
                const delta = Math.floor(-6 + Math.random() * 15);
                aviatorState.displayedPlayerCount = Math.max(85, aviatorState.displayedPlayerCount + delta + (realCount > 0 ? 1 : 0));
            }
            const el = document.getElementById("aviatorLivePlayerCount");
            if (el) el.innerText = aviatorState.displayedPlayerCount.toLocaleString();
        }


        function broadcastRealBetToLiveFeed(panel, amount, status = "ACTIVE", mult = 0, payout = 0) {
            if (!currentUserId) return;
            const docId = `${currentUserId}_P${panel}`;
            db.collection("aviator_live_bets").doc(docId).set({
                uid: currentUserId,
                username: userCustomUsernameToken || "Player",
                panel: panel,
                betAmount: amount,
                status: status,
                multiplier: mult,
                payout: payout,
                updatedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
            }).catch(() => {});
        }


        function removeRealBetFromLiveFeed(panel) {
            if (!currentUserId) return;
            db.collection("aviator_live_bets").doc(`${currentUserId}_P${panel}`).delete().catch(() => {});
        }


        function listenToRealLiveBetsFirestore() {
            db.collection("aviator_live_bets").onSnapshot(snap => {
                const now = Date.now();
                const activeReal = [];
                snap.forEach(doc => {
                    const d = doc.data();
                    const ts = d.updatedAt ? d.updatedAt.toDate().getTime() : now;
                    if (now - ts < 120000) {
                        activeReal.push(d);
                    }
                });
                aviatorState.realLiveFirestoreBets = activeReal;
                updateDynamicPlayerCounter(false);
                renderLiveBetsFeed();
            });
        }


        window.sendAviatorQuickReaction = function(msg) {
            triggerToastDisplay(`${userCustomUsernameToken || "You"}: ${msg}`);
        };


        function generateSmartCrashMultiplier() {
            if (systemSettings.forcedCrashPoint && systemSettings.forcedCrashPoint >= 1.00) {
                const manualVal = parseFloat(systemSettings.forcedCrashPoint);
                systemSettings.forcedCrashPoint = null;
                db.collection("system_settings").doc("game_tax_config").set({ forcedCrashPoint: null }, { merge: true });
                return manualVal;
            }


            const r = Math.random() * 100;
            const mode = systemSettings.aviatorDifficulty || "house_edge";


            if (mode === "strict") {
                if (r < 52) return parseFloat((1.01 + Math.random() * 0.44).toFixed(2));
                if (r < 85) return parseFloat((1.46 + Math.random() * 1.54).toFixed(2));
                if (r < 97) return parseFloat((3.01 + Math.random() * 11.99).toFixed(2));
                if (r < 99.5) return parseFloat((15.01 + Math.random() * 84.99).toFixed(2));
                return parseFloat((100.0 + Math.random() * 400.0).toFixed(2));
            } else if (mode === "generous") {
                if (r < 28) return parseFloat((1.05 + Math.random() * 0.45).toFixed(2));
                if (r < 72) return parseFloat((1.51 + Math.random() * 3.49).toFixed(2));
                if (r < 93) return parseFloat((5.01 + Math.random() * 24.99).toFixed(2));
                if (r < 98.5) return parseFloat((30.01 + Math.random() * 169.99).toFixed(2));
                return parseFloat((200.0 + Math.random() * 800.0).toFixed(2));
            } else {
                let val;
                if (r < 40) val = parseFloat((1.02 + Math.random() * 0.43).toFixed(2));
                else if (r < 78) val = parseFloat((1.46 + Math.random() * 2.54).toFixed(2));
                else if (r < 94) val = parseFloat((4.01 + Math.random() * 15.99).toFixed(2));
                else if (r < 98.8) val = parseFloat((20.01 + Math.random() * 129.99).toFixed(2));
                else val = parseFloat((150.0 + Math.random() * 650.0).toFixed(2));
                const cap = Number(systemSettings.maxCrashCap) || 500;
                return Math.min(cap, val);
            }
        }


        window.adminInstantCrashAviatorNow = function() {
            if (aviatorState.status === "FLYING") {
                aviatorState.crashPoint = aviatorState.currentMultiplier;
                triggerToastDisplay("Triggered instant crash on active Aviator flight!");
            } else {
                systemSettings.forcedCrashPoint = 1.01;
                triggerToastDisplay("Next Aviator flight queued to crash at 1.01x!");
            }
        };


        window.adminTriggerBonusMultiplierRound = function() {
            systemSettings.forcedCrashPoint = 25.00;
            db.collection("system_settings").doc("game_tax_config").set({ forcedCrashPoint: 25.00 }, { merge: true });
            triggerToastDisplay("Queued 25.00x Promo Flight for next round!");
        };


        function playAviatorTone(type) {
            if (!aviatorState.soundEnabled) return;
            try {
                if (!aviatorState.audioCtx) aviatorState.audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
                const ctx = aviatorState.audioCtx;
                if (ctx.state === "suspended") ctx.resume();
                const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
                const gain = ctx.createGain();
                osc.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(ctx.destination);
                const now = ctx.currentTime;


                if (type === "bet") {
                    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(520, now);
                    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(780, now + 0.12);
                    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.08, now);
                    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.14);
                    osc.start(now); osc.stop(now + 0.15);
                } else if (type === "takeoff") {
                    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(220, now);
                    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(620, now + 0.35);
                    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.09, now);
                    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.38);
                    osc.start(now); osc.stop(now + 0.4);
                } else if (type === "cashout") {
                    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(660, now);
                    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, now + 0.09);
                    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.11, now);
                    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.32);
                    osc.start(now); osc.stop(now + 0.34);
                } else if (type === "crash") {
                    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(190, now);
                    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(55, now + 0.42);
                    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, now);
                    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.45);
                    osc.start(now); osc.stop(now + 0.46);
                }
            } catch (e) {}
        }


        window.toggleAviatorSound = function() {
            aviatorState.soundEnabled = !aviatorState.soundEnabled;
            triggerToastDisplay(aviatorState.soundEnabled ? "Game sound enabled" : "Game sound muted");
        };


        window.switchAviatorSubTab = function(tab) {
            const tabs = { live: ["avSubTabLive", "avSectionLive"], mybets: ["avSubTabMyBets", "avSectionMyBets"], stats: ["avSubTabStats", "avSectionStats"] };
            Object.keys(tabs).forEach(k => {
                document.getElementById(tabs[k][0]).classList.toggle("active", k === tab);
                document.getElementById(tabs[k][1]).style.display = k === tab ? "block" : "none";
            });
        };


        window.setAviatorQuickBet = function(panel, amount) {
            const input = document.getElementById(panel === 1 ? "aviatorBetAmountInput" : "aviatorBetAmountInput2");
            if (input) {
                input.value = Math.min(50000, Math.max(10, amount));
                updateAviatorActionButtons();
            }
        };


        window.setAviatorMaxBet = function(panel) {
            const input = document.getElementById(panel === 1 ? "aviatorBetAmountInput" : "aviatorBetAmountInput2");
            if (input) {
                const maxAllowed = Math.floor(Math.min(50000, getTotalPlayableBalance()));
                input.value = maxAllowed >= 10 ? maxAllowed : 10;
                updateAviatorActionButtons();
            }
        };


        window.refreshAviatorPanelPreviews = function() { updateAviatorActionButtons(); };


        window.toggleAutoBetState = function(panel) {
            const isChecked = document.getElementById(`autoBetToggle${panel}`).checked;
            const roundsSelect = parseInt(document.getElementById(`autoBetRounds${panel}`).value) || 5;
            const remLabel = document.getElementById(`autoBetRem${panel}`);
            if (isChecked) {
                aviatorState.panels[panel].autoBetRemaining = roundsSelect;
                remLabel.innerText = `(${roundsSelect} left)`;
                if (aviatorState.status === "BETTING" && !aviatorState.panels[panel].activeBet) {
                    placeBetOnPanel(panel, true);
                }
            } else {
                aviatorState.panels[panel].autoBetRemaining = 0;
                remLabel.innerText = "";
            }
        };


        window.updateAutoBetCount = function(panel) {
            if (document.getElementById(`autoBetToggle${panel}`).checked) {
                const roundsSelect = parseInt(document.getElementById(`autoBetRounds${panel}`).value) || 5;
                aviatorState.panels[panel].autoBetRemaining = roundsSelect;
                document.getElementById(`autoBetRem${panel}`).innerText = `(${roundsSelect} left)`;
            }
        };


        function updateAviatorActionButtons() {
            [1, 2].forEach(panel => {
                const btn = document.getElementById(panel === 1 ? "aviatorActionBtn" : "aviatorActionBtn2");
                const tag = document.getElementById(panel === 1 ? "panel1StatusTag" : "panel2StatusTag");
                const input = document.getElementById(panel === 1 ? "aviatorBetAmountInput" : "aviatorBetAmountInput2");
                if (!btn || !tag) return;


                const pState = aviatorState.panels[panel];
                const inputVal = parseFloat(input && input.value) || (panel === 1 ? 100 : 200);


                if (aviatorState.status === "BETTING") {
                    if (pState.activeBet) {
                        btn.disabled = false;
                        btn.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg, #ef4444, #dc2626)";
                        btn.innerText = `CANCEL BET (PKR ${pState.betAmount.toLocaleString()})`;
                        tag.innerText = "BET ACCEPTED";
                        tag.style.color = "#10b981";
                    } else {
                        btn.disabled = false;
                        btn.style.background = panel === 1 ? "linear-gradient(135deg, #0ea5e9, #0284c7)" : "linear-gradient(135deg, #38bdf8, #0369a1)";
                        btn.innerText = `PLACE BET${panel === 2 ? " 2" : ""} (PKR ${inputVal.toLocaleString()})`;
                        tag.innerText = "BETTING OPEN";
                        tag.style.color = "#0ea5e9";
                    }
                } else if (aviatorState.status === "FLYING") {
                    if (pState.activeBet) {
                        const livePayout = pState.betAmount * aviatorState.currentMultiplier;
                        btn.disabled = false;
                        btn.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg, #10b981, #059669)";
                        btn.innerText = `CASH OUT — PKR ${livePayout.toFixed(2)}`;
                        tag.innerText = `IN FLIGHT @ ${aviatorState.currentMultiplier.toFixed(2)}x`;
                        tag.style.color = "#10b981";
                    } else {
                        btn.disabled = true;
                        btn.style.background = "rgba(100, 116, 139, 0.35)";
                        btn.innerText = "BETTING CLOSED";
                        tag.innerText = "IN FLIGHT";
                        tag.style.color = "#eab308";
                    }
                } else {
                    btn.disabled = true;
                    btn.style.background = "rgba(100, 116, 139, 0.35)";
                    btn.innerText = "ROUND SETTLED";
                    tag.innerText = "CRASHED";
                    tag.style.color = "#ef4444";
                }
            });
        }


        function placeBetOnPanel(panel, isAuto = false) {
            if (!currentUserId) { triggerToastDisplay("Please sign in to play."); return false; }
            const pState = aviatorState.panels[panel];
            const input = document.getElementById(panel === 1 ? "aviatorBetAmountInput" : "aviatorBetAmountInput2");
            const amount = parseFloat(input.value);


            if (isNaN(amount) || amount < 10 || amount > 50000) {
                if (!isAuto) triggerToastDisplay("Bet amount must be between PKR 10 and PKR 50,000.");
                return false;
            }
            if (amount > getTotalPlayableBalance()) {
                document.getElementById("aviatorTopUpPromptBox").style.display = "block";
                if (!isAuto) triggerToastDisplay("Insufficient PKR balance.");
                document.getElementById(`autoBetToggle${panel}`).checked = false;
                pState.autoBetRemaining = 0;
                document.getElementById(`autoBetRem${panel}`).innerText = "";
                return false;
            }


            document.getElementById("aviatorTopUpPromptBox").style.display = "none";
            deductPlayableBalance(amount);


            pState.activeBet = true;
            pState.betAmount = amount;
            playAviatorTone("bet");


            aviatorState.displayedPlayerCount += 1;
            updateDynamicPlayerCounter(false);
            broadcastRealBetToLiveFeed(panel, amount, "ACTIVE", 0, 0);


            if (!isAuto) triggerToastDisplay(`Panel ${panel} bet confirmed: PKR ${amount.toLocaleString()}`);
            updateAviatorActionButtons();
            renderLiveBetsFeed();
            return true;
        }


        function cashoutPanel(panel, isAutoCashout = false) {
            const pState = aviatorState.panels[panel];
            if (!pState.activeBet) return;


            const cashMult = isAutoCashout
                ? parseFloat(document.getElementById(`autoCashoutVal${panel}`).value)
                : aviatorState.currentMultiplier;


            const payout = pState.betAmount * cashMult;
            creditSharedGameWin(payout);
            pState.activeBet = false;


            broadcastRealBetToLiveFeed(panel, pState.betAmount, "WON", cashMult, payout);


            playAviatorTone("cashout");
            triggerToastDisplay(`${isAutoCashout ? "AUTO " : ""}CASHED OUT @ ${cashMult.toFixed(2)}x! +PKR ${payout.toFixed(2)} Cash`);


            aviatorState.personalBetsList.unshift({
                roundId: aviatorState.roundId,
                panel: panel,
                amount: pState.betAmount,
                multiplier: cashMult,
                payout: payout,
                status: "won",
                time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString()
            });
            renderPersonalHistory();
            updateAviatorActionButtons();
            renderLiveBetsFeed();
        }


        window.handleAviatorPanelAction = function(panel) {
            const pState = aviatorState.panels[panel];
            if (aviatorState.status === "BETTING") {
                if (pState.activeBet) {
                    creditSharedGameWin(pState.betAmount);
                    pState.activeBet = false;
                    pState.betAmount = 0;
                    removeRealBetFromLiveFeed(panel);
                    triggerToastDisplay("Bet cancelled & refunded.");
                    updateAviatorActionButtons();
                    renderLiveBetsFeed();
                } else {
                    placeBetOnPanel(panel, false);
                }
            } else if (aviatorState.status === "FLYING" && pState.activeBet) {
                cashoutPanel(panel, false);
            }
        };


        function startClientSideAviatorEngine() {
            generateMassiveSimulatedCrowd();
            renderLiveBetsFeed();


            setInterval(() => {
                updateDynamicPlayerCounter(false);
                if (aviatorState.status === "FLYING") {
                    renderLiveBetsFeed();
                }
            }, 1400);


            setInterval(() => {
                const secElapsed = Math.floor((Date.now() - aviatorState.sessionStartTime) / 1000);
                const mins = String(Math.floor(secElapsed / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
                const secs = String(secElapsed % 60).padStart(2, "0");
                const timerEl = document.getElementById("aviatorSessionTimer");
                if (timerEl) timerEl.innerText = `${mins}:${secs}`;
            }, 1000);


            if (aviatorState.loopInterval) clearInterval(aviatorState.loopInterval);
            aviatorState.loopInterval = setInterval(() => {
                const now = Date.now();


                if (aviatorState.status === "BETTING" && now >= aviatorState.bettingEndsAt) {
                    aviatorState.status = "FLYING";
                    aviatorState.flightStartedAt = now;
                    aviatorState.crashPoint = generateSmartCrashMultiplier();
                    playAviatorTone("takeoff");
                    updateAviatorStageBadge();
                } else if (aviatorState.status === "FLYING") {
                    const elapsed = (now - aviatorState.flightStartedAt) / 1000;
                    const nextMult = 1.0 + (elapsed * 0.14) + (Math.pow(elapsed, 2.15) * 0.045);


                    [1, 2].forEach(panel => {
                        const p = aviatorState.panels[panel];
                        const autoCoOn = document.getElementById(`autoCashoutToggle${panel}`).checked;
                        const autoCoTarget = parseFloat(document.getElementById(`autoCashoutVal${panel}`).value) || 2.00;
                        if (p.activeBet && autoCoOn && nextMult >= autoCoTarget && autoCoTarget <= aviatorState.crashPoint) {
                            cashoutPanel(panel, true);
                        }
                    });


                    aviatorState.currentMultiplier = nextMult;


                    if (aviatorState.currentMultiplier >= aviatorState.crashPoint) {
                        aviatorState.status = "CRASHED";
                        playAviatorTone("crash");


                        [1, 2].forEach(panel => {
                            const p = aviatorState.panels[panel];
                            if (p.activeBet) {
                                aviatorState.personalBetsList.unshift({
                                    roundId: aviatorState.roundId,
                                    panel: panel,
                                    amount: p.betAmount,
                                    multiplier: 0,
                                    payout: 0,
                                    status: "crashed",
                                    time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString()
                                });
                                p.activeBet = false;
                                removeRealBetFromLiveFeed(panel);
                            }
                        });


                        aviatorState.recentHistory.unshift(aviatorState.crashPoint);
                        if (aviatorState.recentHistory.length > 18) aviatorState.recentHistory.pop();
                        renderRecentHistoryTicker();
            if (typeof recalculateYieldProjection === "function") recalculateYieldProjection();
            if (typeof calculateReferralTierPreview === "function") calculateReferralTierPreview();


                        renderPersonalHistory();
                        updateAviatorStageBadge();


                        setTimeout(() => {
                            aviatorState.roundId = "RND-" + Date.now();
                            aviatorState.status = "BETTING";
                            aviatorState.bettingEndsAt = Date.now() + 10000;
                            aviatorState.currentMultiplier = 1.00;
                            generateMassiveSimulatedCrowd();
                            updateAviatorStageBadge();


                            [1, 2].forEach(panel => {
                                const autoToggle = document.getElementById(`autoBetToggle${panel}`);
                                const pState = aviatorState.panels[panel];
                                if (autoToggle && autoToggle.checked && pState.autoBetRemaining > 0) {
                                    const placed = placeBetOnPanel(panel, true);
                                    if (placed) {
                                        pState.autoBetRemaining--;
                                        document.getElementById(`autoBetRem${panel}`).innerText = pState.autoBetRemaining > 0 ? `(${pState.autoBetRemaining} left)` : "";
                                        if (pState.autoBetRemaining <= 0) autoToggle.checked = false;
                                    }
                                }
                            });
                            renderLiveBetsFeed();
                        }, 3800);
                    }
                }
            }, 80);


            const renderFrame = () => {
                renderAviatorCanvasUI();
                aviatorState.renderRaf = requestAnimationFrame(renderFrame);
            };
            aviatorState.renderRaf = requestAnimationFrame(renderFrame);
        }


        function renderAviatorCanvasUI() {
            const multEl = document.getElementById("aviatorMultiplierDisplay");
            const subEl = document.getElementById("aviatorSubBannerText");
            const statusEl = document.getElementById("aviatorStatusText");
            const barWrap = document.getElementById("aviatorCountdownBarWrap");
            const barFill = document.getElementById("aviatorCountdownBarFill");
            const planeEl = document.getElementById("aviatorPlaneIcon");
            const curvePath = document.getElementById("aviatorFlightCurvePath");
            const areaPath = document.getElementById("aviatorFlightAreaPath");


            if (!multEl) return;
            const now = Date.now();


            if (aviatorState.status === "BETTING") {
                const remainingMs = Math.max(0, aviatorState.bettingEndsAt - now);
                const remainingSec = (remainingMs / 1000).toFixed(1);
                const pct = Math.min(100, (remainingMs / 10000) * 100);


                subEl.innerText = remainingMs > 0 ? "BETTING OPEN — TAKING OFF IN" : "TAKING OFF...";
                subEl.style.color = "#38bdf8";
                multEl.innerText = `${remainingSec}s`;
                multEl.style.color = "#ffffff";
                statusEl.innerText = "Set Auto-Cashout or place bets before takeoff";
                barWrap.style.display = "block";
                barFill.style.width = `${pct}%`;
                planeEl.style.display = "none";
                curvePath.setAttribute("d", "");
                areaPath.setAttribute("d", "");
            } else if (aviatorState.status === "FLYING") {
                multEl.innerText = `${aviatorState.currentMultiplier.toFixed(2)}x`;
                multEl.style.color = aviatorState.currentMultiplier >= 50 ? "#10b981" : (aviatorState.currentMultiplier >= 5 ? "#eab308" : "#38bdf8");
                subEl.innerText = "IN FLIGHT";
                statusEl.innerText = "Cash out before plane flies away!";
                barWrap.style.display = "none";
                updateAviatorActionButtons();


                const progress = Math.min(1, (now - aviatorState.flightStartedAt) / 9000);
                const endX = 24 + progress * 310;
                const endY = 220 - Math.pow(progress, 1.35) * 175;
                curvePath.setAttribute("d", `M 24 220 Q ${24 + (endX-24)*0.5} 220 ${endX} ${endY}`);
                areaPath.setAttribute("d", `M 24 220 Q ${24 + (endX-24)*0.5} 220 ${endX} ${endY} L ${endX} 250 L 24 250 Z`);


                planeEl.style.display = "block";
                planeEl.style.left = `${(endX / 400) * 100}%`;
                planeEl.style.bottom = `${((250 - endY) / 250) * 100}%`;
            } else if (aviatorState.status === "CRASHED") {
                multEl.innerText = `${aviatorState.crashPoint.toFixed(2)}x`;
                multEl.style.color = "#ef4444";
                subEl.innerText = "FLEW AWAY!";
                statusEl.innerText = "Preparing next round...";
            }
        }


        function updateAviatorStageBadge() {
            const badge = document.getElementById("aviatorRoundStateBadge");
            if (!badge) return;
            if (aviatorState.status === "BETTING") {
                badge.className = "aviator-status-pill aviator-status-open";
                badge.innerText = "BETTING OPEN";
            } else if (aviatorState.status === "FLYING") {
                badge.className = "aviator-status-pill aviator-status-closed";
                badge.innerText = "IN FLIGHT";
            } else {
                badge.className = "aviator-status-pill aviator-status-crashed";
                badge.innerText = "CRASHED";
            }
            updateAviatorActionButtons();
        }


        function renderRecentHistoryTicker() {
            const tickerBar = document.getElementById("aviatorMultiplierTickerBar");
            if (!tickerBar) return;
            let html = "";
            aviatorState.recentHistory.forEach(val => {
                const cls = val >= 50 ? "aviator-pill-mega" : (val >= 5 ? "aviator-pill-high" : (val >= 2 ? "aviator-pill-mid" : "aviator-pill-low"));
                html += `<span class="aviator-pill ${cls}">${val.toFixed(2)}x</span>`;
            });
            tickerBar.innerHTML = html;
        }


        function renderLiveBetsFeed() {
            const feedEl = document.getElementById("aviatorLivePlayersFeed");
            if (!feedEl) return;
            let html = "";
            let totalShown = 0;


            [1, 2].forEach(p => {
                if (aviatorState.panels[p].activeBet) {
                    totalShown++;
                    html += `<div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns: 2.2fr 1.2fr 1fr 1.4fr; font-size:11px; padding:6px 4px; background:rgba(14,165,233,0.16); border:1px solid rgba(56,189,248,0.35); border-radius:8px; text-align:center;">
                        <span style="color:var(--sky-primary); font-weight:800; text-align:left;">${userCustomUsernameToken} (P${p}) <span class="live-badge-real">YOU</span></span>
                        <span style="color:var(--text-main); font-weight:700;">${aviatorState.panels[p].betAmount.toLocaleString()}</span>
                        <span style="color:#eab308;">--</span>
                        <span style="color:#10b981; font-weight:800;">IN PLAY</span>
                    </div>`;
                }
            });


            aviatorState.realLiveFirestoreBets.forEach(rb => {
                if (rb.uid === currentUserId) return;
                totalShown++;
                const isWon = rb.status === "WON";
                html += `<div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns: 2.2fr 1.2fr 1fr 1.4fr; font-size:11px; padding:6px 4px; background:rgba(16,185,129,0.12); border:1px solid rgba(16,185,129,0.3); border-radius:8px; text-align:center;">
                    <span style="color:#10b981; font-weight:800; text-align:left;">${rb.username} <span class="live-badge-real">LIVE</span></span>
                    <span style="color:var(--text-main); font-weight:700;">${Number(rb.betAmount).toLocaleString()}</span>
                    <span style="color:${isWon ? '#10b981' : '#eab308'}; font-weight:700;">${isWon ? Number(rb.multiplier).toFixed(2) + 'x' : '--'}</span>
                    <span style="color:${isWon ? '#10b981' : '#0ea5e9'}; font-weight:800;">${isWon ? '+' + Number(rb.payout).toFixed(0) : 'Playing'}</span>
                </div>`;
            });


            aviatorState.simulatedBots.forEach(b => {
                totalShown++;
                const won = aviatorState.status !== "BETTING" && aviatorState.currentMultiplier >= b.targetMult && b.targetMult <= aviatorState.crashPoint;
                const rowBg = won ? "rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.08)" : "transparent";
                html += `<div style="display:grid; grid-template-columns: 2.2fr 1.2fr 1fr 1.4fr; font-size:11px; padding:5px 4px; background:${rowBg}; border-radius:6px; text-align:center; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-color);">
                    <span style="color:var(--text-main); text-align:left; font-weight:600;">${b.name}</span>
                    <span style="color:var(--text-muted);">${b.bet.toLocaleString()}</span>
                    <span style="color:${won ? '#10b981' : 'var(--text-muted)'}; font-weight:700;">${won ? b.targetMult.toFixed(2) + 'x' : '--'}</span>
                    <span style="color:${won ? '#10b981' : 'var(--sky-primary)'}; font-weight:700;">${won ? '+' + Math.round(b.bet * b.targetMult).toLocaleString() : 'Betting'}</span>
                </div>`;
            });


            const badgeEl = document.getElementById("liveBetsTotalBadge");
            if (badgeEl) badgeEl.innerText = totalShown;
            feedEl.innerHTML = html;
        }


        function renderPersonalHistory() {
            const listEl = document.getElementById("aviatorPersonalHistoryList");
            if (!listEl) return;
            let html = "";
            aviatorState.personalBetsList.forEach(b => {
                let badgeColor = b.status === "won" ? "#10b981" : "#ef4444";
                html += `<div style="background:var(--bg-sub); border:1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius:12px; padding:10px; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; font-size:11px;">
                    <div>
                        <div style="font-weight:800; color:var(--text-main);">Panel ${b.panel} <span style="color:var(--text-muted);">(${b.time})</span></div>
                        <div style="color:var(--text-muted); margin-top:2px;">Bet: <b>PKR ${b.amount}</b> | Cashout: <b>${b.multiplier > 0 ? b.multiplier.toFixed(2) + "x" : "--"}</b></div>
                    </div>
                    <div style="font-weight:800; color:${badgeColor};">${b.status === "won" ? "+PKR " + b.payout.toFixed(2) : "CRASHED"}</div>
                </div>`;
            });
            listEl.innerHTML = html || `<div style="color:var(--text-muted); text-align:center; padding:14px; font-size:11px;">No personal bets recorded yet.</div>`;
            document.getElementById("statTotalRounds").innerText = String(aviatorState.personalBetsList.length);
            let maxW = aviatorState.personalBetsList.reduce((acc, curr) => Math.max(acc, curr.payout), 0);
            document.getElementById("statBiggestWin").innerText = `PKR ${maxW.toFixed(2)}`;
        }


        /* =========================================================================
           VIP 5,000 PKR MILESTONE SPIN
           ========================================================================= */
        function updateAviatorMilestoneUI() {
            const btn = document.getElementById("btnClaimAviatorSpin");
            const bar = document.getElementById("aviatorSpinProgressBar");
            if (!btn || !bar) return;


            const pct = Math.min(100, (aviatorWagerProgress / 5000) * 100);
            bar.style.width = `${pct}%`;


            if (aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable > 0) {
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-gift fa-bounce"></i> SPIN NOW (${aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable})`;
            } else {
                btn.disabled = true;
                btn.innerHTML = `<i class="fa-solid fa-lock"></i> ${Math.floor(aviatorWagerProgress).toLocaleString()} / 5,000`;
            }
        }


        function refreshMilestoneModalButtons() {
            const countLbl = document.getElementById("milestoneModalSpinsCount");
            const spinAllBtn = document.getElementById("btnExecuteAviatorMilestoneSpinAll");
            const spinAllCount = document.getElementById("spinAllBtnCount");
            if (countLbl) countLbl.innerText = String(aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable);
            if (spinAllCount) spinAllCount.innerText = String(aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable);
            if (spinAllBtn) {
                spinAllBtn.style.display = aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable > 1 ? "block" : "none";
            }
        }


        window.openAviatorMilestoneSpinModal = function() {
            if (aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable <= 0) {
                triggerToastDisplay("Play Rs. 5,000 in games to unlock a free spin!");
                return;
            }
            refreshMilestoneModalButtons();
            openModal("aviatorMilestoneSpinModal");
        };


        function rollSingleSafeMilestoneReward() {
            // Exact original company-safe bonus tiers & weights
            const actualBonusTiers = [0, 5, 10, 15, 25, 40, 60, 80, 100];
            const weights = [10, 20, 22, 18, 12, 8, 5, 2, 1];
            let rand = Math.random() * 100, sum = 0, rewardAmount = 10;
            for (let i = 0; i < actualBonusTiers.length; i++) {
                sum += weights[i];
                if (rand <= sum) { rewardAmount = actualBonusTiers[i]; break; }
            }
            return rewardAmount;
        }


        window.executeAviatorMilestoneSpin = function(spinAll = false) {
            if (aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable <= 0) return;
            const btn1 = document.getElementById("btnExecuteAviatorMilestoneSpin");
            const btnAll = document.getElementById("btnExecuteAviatorMilestoneSpinAll");
            const wheel = document.getElementById("aviatorMilestoneWheel");
            const centerTxt = document.getElementById("milestoneWheelCenterText");
            if (btn1) btn1.disabled = true;
            if (btnAll) btnAll.disabled = true;


            const spinsToUse = spinAll ? aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable : 1;
            let totalReward = 0;
            let breakdown = [];
            for (let s = 0; s < spinsToUse; s++) {
                const r = rollSingleSafeMilestoneReward();
                totalReward += r;
                breakdown.push(`Rs.${r}`);
            }


            if (wheel) wheel.style.transform = spinAll ? "rotate(2520deg)" : "rotate(1800deg)";
            if (centerTxt) centerTxt.innerHTML = spinAll ? `SPINNING<br>${spinsToUse}x...` : `SPINNING...`;


            setTimeout(() => {
                aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable = Math.max(0, aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable - spinsToUse);
                userBonusBalance = Number((userBonusBalance + totalReward).toFixed(2));
                updateWalletUI();
                updateAviatorMilestoneUI();
                refreshMilestoneModalButtons();


                db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId).update({
                    aviatorFreeSpins: aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable,
                    bonusBalance: userBonusBalance,
                    balance: getTotalPlayableBalance()
                }).then(() => {
                    const summaryMsg = spinsToUse > 1
                        ? `Used ${spinsToUse} Spins! Won Total +Rs. ${totalReward.toLocaleString()} Bonus (${breakdown.slice(0, 6).join(", ")}${breakdown.length > 6 ? "..." : ""})`
                        : `VIP Milestone Spin Won +Rs. ${totalReward.toLocaleString()} Bonus!`;
                    triggerToastDisplay(summaryMsg);
                    if (typeof pushInAppNotification === "function") {
                        pushInAppNotification("VIP Milestone Spin Reward", summaryMsg, "promo");
                    }
                    if (wheel) wheel.style.transform = "rotate(0deg)";
                    if (centerTxt) centerTxt.innerHTML = `100 - 50000<br>PKR`;
                    if (btn1) btn1.disabled = false;
                    if (btnAll) btnAll.disabled = false;
                    if (aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable <= 0) {
                        closeModal("aviatorMilestoneSpinModal");
                    }
                });
            }, spinAll ? 1800 : 3000);
        };
