        auth.onAuthStateChanged(user => {
            if (user) {
                currentUserId = user.uid;
                document.getElementById('authScreen').style.display = 'none';
                syncProfileRealtimeMetrics();
                syncUserActiveLeasesList();
                syncUserLedgerTracks();
                syncUserSupportTickets();
                listenToUserNotifications(user.uid);
                initializeCountdownClockTicker();
                updateLivePresenceActivity("Home View");
                renderSavedPayoutAccountCard();
            } else {
                currentUserId = null;
                document.getElementById('authScreen').style.display = 'flex';
            }
        });


        function updateWalletUI() {
            const total = getTotalPlayableBalance();
            const fmtTotal = total.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
            document.getElementById('assetDisplay').innerText = fmtTotal;
            document.getElementById('withdrawableBalanceDisplay').innerText = userWithdrawableBalance.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
            document.getElementById('bonusBalanceDisplay').innerText = userBonusBalance.toLocaleString('en-US', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
            document.getElementById('aviatorWalletBalanceLbl').innerText = fmtTotal;
            const hubLbl = document.getElementById('hubSharedBalanceLbl');
            if (hubLbl) hubLbl.innerText = fmtTotal;
            document.getElementById('avCashSubLbl').innerText = userWithdrawableBalance.toFixed(0);
            document.getElementById('avBonusSubLbl').innerText = userBonusBalance.toFixed(0);
            updateAviatorMilestoneUI();
        }


        function updateLivePresenceActivity(pageName) {
            if(!currentUserId) return;
            db.collection("presence").doc(currentUserId).set({
                uid: currentUserId,
                username: userCustomUsernameToken || "User",
                currentPage: pageName,
                lastActive: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
            }, { merge: true });
        }


        window.switchAuthTab = function(mode) {
            currentAuthTabMode = mode;
            document.getElementById('tabLogin').classList.toggle('active', mode === 'login');
            document.getElementById('tabRegister').classList.toggle('active', mode === 'register');
            document.getElementById('authUsername').style.display = mode === 'register' ? 'block' : 'none';
        };


        window.handleAuthAction = function() {
            const email = document.getElementById('authEmail').value.trim();
            const pass = document.getElementById('authPassword').value;
            const username = document.getElementById('authUsername').value.trim().toLowerCase();
            const sponsorCode = document.getElementById('authReferralInput').value.trim().toLowerCase();


            if(!email || !pass) { triggerToastDisplay("Please enter required email and password."); return; }


            if(currentAuthTabMode === "register") {
                if(!username) { triggerToastDisplay("Please choose a unique username."); return; }
                
                db.collection("users").where("username", "==", username).get().then(snap => {
                    if(!snap.empty) { throw new Error("Username is already taken."); }
                    return auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(email, pass);
                }).then(cred => {
                    let userPayload = {
                        uid: cred.user.uid,
                        balance: 0.00,
                        withdrawableBalance: 0.00,
                        bonusBalance: 0.00,
                        aviatorWagerProgress: 0.00,
                        aviatorFreeSpins: 0,
                        minersCount: 0,
                        email: email,
                        username: username,
                        profilePic: "",
                        isBlocked: false,
                        hasSpunToday: false,
                        hasCheckedInToday: false,
                        completedFbTask: false,
                        referredBy: sponsorCode || "none",
                        hasDepositedBefore: false,
                        totalYieldEarned: 0.00,
                        multiTierEarned: 0.00,
                        timestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
                        lastYieldSyncTimestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
                        lastUsernameChangeDate: null
                    };
                    return db.collection("users").doc(cred.user.uid).set(userPayload);
                }).then(() => {
                    triggerToastDisplay("Account Created Successfully!");
                }).catch(e => triggerToastDisplay(e.message || e));
            } else {
                auth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(email, pass)
                    .then(cred => {
                        db.collection("users").doc(cred.user.uid).get().then(doc => {
                            if(doc.exists && doc.data().isBlocked) {
                                auth.signOut();
                                triggerToastDisplay("Account Blocked: Contact Support.");
                            } else {
                                triggerToastDisplay("Logged In Successfully!");
                            }
                        });
                    })
                    .catch(e => triggerToastDisplay(e.message));
            }
        };


        window.executeGoogleAuthenticationLink = function() {
            const provider = new firebase.auth.GoogleAuthProvider();
            auth.signInWithPopup(provider).then(result => {
                const user = result.user;
                const userRef = db.collection("users").doc(user.uid);
                
                userRef.get().then(doc => {
                    if(!doc.exists) {
                        let computedRandomUsername = "user_" + user.email.split('@')[0] + Math.floor(Math.random()*100);
                        const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
                        let sponsorCode = params.get('ref') || "none";


                        userRef.set({
                            uid: user.uid,
                            balance: 0.00,
                            withdrawableBalance: 0.00,
                            bonusBalance: 0.00,
                            aviatorWagerProgress: 0.00,
                            aviatorFreeSpins: 0,
                            minersCount: 0,
                            email: user.email,
                            username: computedRandomUsername,
                            profilePic: user.photoURL || "",
                            isBlocked: false,
                            hasSpunToday: false,
                            hasCheckedInToday: false,
                            completedFbTask: false,
                            referredBy: sponsorCode,
                            hasDepositedBefore: false,
                            totalYieldEarned: 0.00,
                            multiTierEarned: 0.00,
                            timestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
                            lastYieldSyncTimestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                        }).then(() => triggerToastDisplay("Google Sign In Successful!"));
                    } else {
                        if(doc.data().isBlocked) {
                            auth.signOut();
                            triggerToastDisplay("Account Blocked.");
                            return;
                        }
                        triggerToastDisplay("Welcome Back!");
                    }
                });
            }).catch(err => triggerToastDisplay(err.message));
        };


        window.triggerSignOut = function() { auth.signOut(); };


        function syncProfileRealtimeMetrics() {
            db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId).onSnapshot(doc => {
                if(doc.exists) {
                    const d = doc.data();
                    if(d.isBlocked) {
                        auth.signOut();
                        triggerToastDisplay("Account is blocked by admin.");
                        return;
                    }


                    if (d.withdrawableBalance === undefined && d.bonusBalance === undefined) {
                        userWithdrawableBalance = d.balance || 0.00;
                        userBonusBalance = 0.00;
                    } else {
                        userWithdrawableBalance = d.withdrawableBalance || 0.00;
                        userBonusBalance = d.bonusBalance || 0.00;
                    }


                    aviatorWagerProgress = d.aviatorWagerProgress || 0.00;
                    aviatorFreeSpinsAvailable = d.aviatorFreeSpins || 0;
                    totalActiveCoresCount = d.minersCount || 0;
                    userCustomUsernameToken = d.username || "User";
                    userProfilePicBase64 = d.profilePic || "";
                    lastUsernameChangeDate = d.lastUsernameChangeDate ? d.lastUsernameChangeDate.toDate() : null;
                    
                    updateWalletUI();
                    document.getElementById('minerDisplay').innerText = `${totalActiveCoresCount} Nodes`;
                    document.getElementById('lblHeaderUsername').innerText = userCustomUsernameToken;
                    
                    if(userProfilePicBase64) {
                        document.getElementById('userAvatarImage').src = userProfilePicBase64;
                        document.getElementById('modalProfilePreviewImg').src = userProfilePicBase64;
                    }


                    document.getElementById('lblTotalYieldGenerated').innerText = "Rs. " + (d.totalYieldEarned || 0.00).toLocaleString('en-US',{minimumFractionDigits:2});
                    document.getElementById('lblMultiTierTotalEarning').innerText = (d.multiTierEarned || 0.00).toLocaleString('en-US',{minimumFractionDigits:2});


                    let calculatedLevel = "Level 1 Member";
                    if(totalActiveCoresCount >= 10 || getTotalPlayableBalance() >= 20000) calculatedLevel = "Diamond VIP Tier";
                    else if(totalActiveCoresCount >= 5 || getTotalPlayableBalance() >= 5000) calculatedLevel = "Gold Executive Tier";
                    document.getElementById('lblUserLevelBadge').innerText = calculatedLevel;


                    let rootOriginLink = window.location.origin + window.location.pathname;
                    document.getElementById('lblDisplayInviteLink').innerText = `${rootOriginLink}?ref=${userCustomUsernameToken}`;
                    
                    if(d.hasCheckedInToday) {
                        document.getElementById('btnDailyCheckin').innerText = "Claimed";
                        document.getElementById('btnDailyCheckin').disabled = true;
                    }
                    if(d.completedFbTask) {
                        document.getElementById('btnFbTask').innerText = "Done";
                        document.getElementById('btnFbTask').disabled = true;
                    }


                    calculateMultiTierNetworkHierarchy(userCustomUsernameToken);
                    executeProfessionalLazyYieldSync(currentUserId, d);
                }
            });
        }


        function syncUserActiveLeasesList() {
            db.collection("leases").where("userId", "==", currentUserId).onSnapshot(snap => {
                const box = document.getElementById("myActiveNodesOverview");
                const chips = document.getElementById("myActiveNodesChips");
                const summary = document.getElementById("myDailyReturnSummary");
                if (!box || !chips || !summary) return;


                if (snap.empty) {
                    box.style.display = "none";
                    updateDetailedLeasesTracker(snap);
                    return;
                }


                let totalDaily = 0;
                let html = "";
                snap.forEach(doc => {
                    const l = doc.data();
                    totalDaily += (Number(l.dailyYield) || 0);
                    html += `<span style="background:var(--bg-card); border:1px solid var(--border-color); font-size:10px; padding:4px 8px; border-radius:8px; font-weight:700; color:var(--text-main);">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-bolt" style="color:#0ea5e9;"></i> ${l.planName} (+Rs.${l.dailyYield}/d)
                    </span>`;
                });


                box.style.display = "block";
                chips.innerHTML = html;
                summary.innerText = `+Rs. ${totalDaily.toLocaleString()}/day`;
                updateDetailedLeasesTracker(snap);
            });
        }


        function executeProfessionalLazyYieldSync(uid, userData) {
            const lastSyncDate = userData.lastYieldSyncTimestamp ? userData.lastYieldSyncTimestamp.toDate() : null;
            const currentTime = new Date();
            if (!lastSyncDate) return;


            const elapsedMilliseconds = currentTime - lastSyncDate;
            const absolutePassedDays = Math.floor(elapsedMilliseconds / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));


            if (absolutePassedDays >= 1) {
                db.collection("leases").where("userId", "==", uid).get().then(snapshot => {
                    if (snapshot.empty) {
                        db.collection("users").doc(uid).update({
                            lastYieldSyncTimestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
                            hasCheckedInToday: false
                        });
                        return;
                    }


                    let cumulativeProfitPerDay = 0;
                    snapshot.forEach(leaseDoc => {
                        cumulativeProfitPerDay += (leaseDoc.data().dailyYield || 0);
                    });


                    let globalCalculatedProfitToken = cumulativeProfitPerDay * absolutePassedDays;


                    if (globalCalculatedProfitToken > 0) {
                        const operationalBatch = db.batch();
                        const userRef = db.collection("users").doc(uid);


                        operationalBatch.update(userRef, {
                            withdrawableBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(globalCalculatedProfitToken),
                            balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(globalCalculatedProfitToken),
                            totalYieldEarned: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(globalCalculatedProfitToken),
                            lastYieldSyncTimestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
                            hasSpunToday: false,
                            hasCheckedInToday: false
                        });


                        const logRef = db.collection("deposits").doc();
                        operationalBatch.set(logRef, {
                            userId: uid,
                            amount: globalCalculatedProfitToken,
                            transactionId: "PROFIT-" + Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000),
                            gatewayChannel: `Mining Node Profit (${absolutePassedDays} Days)`,
                            status: "approved",
                            timestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                        });


                        operationalBatch.commit().then(() => {
                            triggerToastDisplay(`+Rs. ${globalCalculatedProfitToken} Withdrawable Mining Profit Credited!`);
                        });
                    }
                });
            }
        }


        /* =========================================================================
           DYNAMIC MINING NODES RENDERER & PURCHASE
           ========================================================================= */
        function initializeRealtimeNodeConfigSync() {
            db.collection("node_configs").onSnapshot(snapshot => {
                activeNodesMap = {};
                defaultNodesCatalog.forEach(n => { activeNodesMap[n.nodeId] = { ...n }; });


                snapshot.forEach(doc => {
                    const data = doc.data();
                    const id = data.nodeId || doc.id;
                    if (data.isDeleted) {
                        delete activeNodesMap[id];
                    } else {
                        activeNodesMap[id] = {
                            nodeId: id,
                            category: data.category || "normal",
                            cost: Number(data.cost) || 200,
                            dailyYield: Number(data.dailyYield) || 16,
                            duration: Number(data.duration) || 30,
                            imageUrl: data.imageUrl || "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1639762681485-074b7f938ba0?w=120&auto=format&fit=crop&q=60"
                        };
                    }
                });


                renderAllNodeCards();
                renderAdminNodesList();
            });
        }


        function renderAllNodeCards() {
            const gNorm = document.getElementById("gridNormal");
            const gSpec = document.getElementById("gridSpecial");
            const gOffr = document.getElementById("gridOffers");
            if (!gNorm || !gSpec || !gOffr) return;


            gNorm.innerHTML = "";
            gSpec.innerHTML = "";
            gOffr.innerHTML = "";


            Object.values(activeNodesMap).forEach(node => {
                const isSpec = node.category === "special";
                const isOffr = node.category === "offer";
                const borderClass = isSpec ? "special-border" : (isOffr ? "offer-border" : "");
                const btnStyle = isSpec
                    ? "background: linear-gradient(135deg, #eab308, #ca8a04);"
                    : (isOffr ? "background: linear-gradient(135deg, #a855f7, #7c3aed);" : "");
                const iconHtml = isSpec
                    ? `<i class="fa-solid fa-crown" style="color:#eab308; font-size:10px;"></i>`
                    : (isOffr ? `<i class="fa-solid fa-bolt-lightning" style="color:#a855f7; font-size:10px;"></i>` : `<i class="fa-solid fa-circle-check" style="color:#0ea5e9; font-size:10px;"></i>`);


                const cardHtml = `<div class="plan-card ${borderClass}">
                    <img src="${node.imageUrl}" class="plan-node-img" alt="Node">
                    <div class="plan-details">
                        <h4>${node.nodeId} ${iconHtml}</h4>
                        <p>Price: <b>Rs. ${node.cost.toLocaleString()}</b> | Daily: <span>Rs. ${node.dailyYield}/Day</span></p>
                        <p style="color:var(--text-muted); font-size:10px;">Duration: ${node.duration} Days</p>
                    </div>
                    <button class="buy-plan-btn" style="${btnStyle}" onclick="leaseHardwareNode('${node.nodeId.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', ${node.cost}, ${node.dailyYield}, ${node.duration})">Activate</button>
                </div>`;


                if (isSpec) gSpec.innerHTML += cardHtml;
                else if (isOffr) gOffr.innerHTML += cardHtml;
                else gNorm.innerHTML += cardHtml;
            });
        }


        window.leaseHardwareNode = function(name, price, dailyProfitValue, durationDays = 30) {
            if(getTotalPlayableBalance() < price) {
                triggerToastDisplay("Insufficient balance for this node.");
                return;
            }
            
            let fromBonus = Math.min(userBonusBalance, price);
            let fromWithdrawable = price - fromBonus;
            userBonusBalance -= fromBonus;
            userWithdrawableBalance -= fromWithdrawable;


            const operationalBatch = db.batch();
            const userRef = db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId);
            
            operationalBatch.update(userRef, {
                bonusBalance: userBonusBalance,
                withdrawableBalance: userWithdrawableBalance,
                balance: getTotalPlayableBalance(),
                minersCount: totalActiveCoresCount + 1,
                hasDepositedBefore: true
            });


            const newLeaseRef = db.collection("leases").doc();
            operationalBatch.set(newLeaseRef, {
                userId: currentUserId,
                planName: name,
                cost: price,
                dailyYield: dailyProfitValue,
                durationDays: durationDays,
                timestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
            });


            operationalBatch.commit().then(() => {
                triggerToastDisplay(`Activated ${name} successfully! Daily profit goes to Withdrawable Cash.`);
            }).catch(err => triggerToastDisplay(err.message || err));
        };


        /* =========================================================================
           MULTI-TIER REFERRALS & COMMISSIONS (TIER 1, 2 & 3)
           ========================================================================= */
        let activeReferralListenerUnsub = null;
        function calculateMultiTierNetworkHierarchy(userUsername) {
            if (!userUsername) return;
            const normalizedUser = String(userUsername).trim().toLowerCase();
            if (activeReferralListenerUnsub) {
                try { activeReferralListenerUnsub(); } catch (_) {}
            }
            activeReferralListenerUnsub = db.collection("users").onSnapshot(allSnap => {
                const tier1Docs = [];
                const tier1Usernames = new Set();
                let activeDepositorsCount = 0;


                allSnap.forEach(doc => {
                    const u = doc.data();
                    const refBy = String(u.referredBy || "").trim().toLowerCase();
                    if (refBy && refBy !== "none" && refBy === normalizedUser && doc.id !== currentUserId) {
                        tier1Docs.push({ id: doc.id, data: u });
                        if (u.username) tier1Usernames.add(String(u.username).trim().toLowerCase());
                        if (u.hasDepositedBefore) activeDepositorsCount++;
                    }
                });


                let tier2Count = 0;
                if (tier1Usernames.size > 0) {
                    allSnap.forEach(doc => {
                        const u = doc.data();
                        const refBy = String(u.referredBy || "").trim().toLowerCase();
                        if (refBy && tier1Usernames.has(refBy) && doc.id !== currentUserId) {
                            tier2Count++;
                        }
                    });
                }


                const directCount = tier1Docs.length;
                const cntEl = document.getElementById('cntTotalRefers');
                const actEl = document.getElementById('cntActiveDepositors');
                if (cntEl) cntEl.innerText = `${directCount} Users` + (tier2Count > 0 ? ` (+${tier2Count} T2)` : "");
                if (actEl) actEl.innerText = `${activeDepositorsCount} Active`;


                let weeklyPct = Math.min((activeDepositorsCount / 10) * 100, 100);
                const wBar = document.getElementById('barWeeklyProgress');
                const wTxt = document.getElementById('lblWeeklyProgressTxt');
                if (wBar) wBar.style.width = `${weeklyPct}%`;
                if (wTxt) wTxt.innerText = `Progress: ${activeDepositorsCount} / 10`;


                let monthlyPct = Math.min((activeDepositorsCount / 50) * 100, 100);
                const mBar = document.getElementById('barMonthlyProgress');
                const mTxt = document.getElementById('lblMonthlyProgressTxt');
                if (mBar) mBar.style.width = `${monthlyPct}%`;
                if (mTxt) mTxt.innerText = `Progress: ${activeDepositorsCount} / 50`;


                renderDirectReferralTeamList(tier1Docs);
            });
        }


        function processMultiTierReferralBonus(depositUserId, depositAmount) {
            db.collection("users").doc(depositUserId).get().then(userDoc => {
                if(!userDoc.exists) return;
                let level1Referrer = userDoc.data().referredBy;
                if(!level1Referrer || level1Referrer === "none") return;


                db.collection("users").where("username", "==", level1Referrer).get().then(snapL1 => {
                    if(snapL1.empty) return;
                    let docL1 = snapL1.docs[0];
                    let uidL1 = docL1.id;
                    let rewardL1 = depositAmount * 0.10;


                    db.collection("users").doc(uidL1).update({
                        withdrawableBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(rewardL1),
                        balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(rewardL1),
                        multiTierEarned: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(rewardL1)
                    });


                    db.collection("deposits").add({
                        userId: uidL1, amount: rewardL1, transactionId: "COMM-T1-" + Math.floor(100000 + Math.random()*900000),
                        gatewayChannel: `Tier 1 Commission`, status: "approved", timestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                    });


                    let level2Referrer = docL1.data().referredBy;
                    if(!level2Referrer || level2Referrer === "none") return;


                    db.collection("users").where("username", "==", level2Referrer).get().then(snapL2 => {
                        if(snapL2.empty) return;
                        let docL2 = snapL2.docs[0];
                        let uidL2 = docL2.id;
                        let rewardL2 = depositAmount * 0.03;


                        db.collection("users").doc(uidL2).update({
                            withdrawableBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(rewardL2),
                            balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(rewardL2),
                            multiTierEarned: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(rewardL2)
                        });


                        let level3Referrer = docL2.data().referredBy;
                        if(!level3Referrer || level3Referrer === "none") return;


                        db.collection("users").where("username", "==", level3Referrer).get().then(snapL3 => {
                            if(snapL3.empty) return;
                            let uidL3 = snapL3.docs[0].id;
                            let rewardL3 = depositAmount * 0.01;


                            db.collection("users").doc(uidL3).update({
                                withdrawableBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(rewardL3),
                                balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(rewardL3),
                                multiTierEarned: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(rewardL3)
                            });
                        });
                    });
                });
            });
        }


        window.copyUserReferralLink = function() {
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(document.getElementById('lblDisplayInviteLink').innerText);
            triggerToastDisplay("Referral link copied!");
        };

        window.updateDepositCalculations = function() {
            let amt = parseFloat(document.getElementById('pkrDepositInput').value) || 0;
            db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId).get().then(doc => {
                let hasDepositedBefore = doc.exists ? (doc.data().hasDepositedBefore || false) : false;
                let bonusRate = hasDepositedBefore ? 0.04 : 0.08;
                let bonusLabelText = hasDepositedBefore ? "4% Play Bonus" : "8% First Deposit Bonus";
                let bonus = amt * bonusRate;
                let total = amt + bonus;


                document.getElementById('lblBonusPKR').innerText = `Rs. ${bonus.toLocaleString()} (${bonusLabelText})`;
                document.getElementById('lblTotalCreditedPKR').innerText = "Rs. " + total.toLocaleString();
                document.getElementById('txtPayableExactAmount').innerText = "Rs. " + amt.toLocaleString();
            });
        };


        function loadPaymentMethodsFromFirestore() {
            db.collection("system_settings").doc("payment_gateways").onSnapshot(doc => {
                if(doc.exists) {
                    let data = doc.data();
                    if(data.EasyPaisa) paymentGatewayMerchantConfig.EasyPaisa = data.EasyPaisa;
                    if(data.JazzCash) paymentGatewayMerchantConfig.JazzCash = data.JazzCash;
                    if(data.USDT) paymentGatewayMerchantConfig.USDT = data.USDT;
                    selectDepositGatewayChannel(activeGatewayChannel);
                }
            });
        }


        window.copyMerchantAccountToken = function() {

            triggerToastDisplay("Account details copied!");
        };


        window.submitPkrDepositToFirebase = function() {
            const inputVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById('pkrDepositInput').value);
            const tid = document.getElementById('depositTxIDInput').value.trim();


            if(isNaN(inputVal) || inputVal < 200) { triggerToastDisplay("Minimum deposit amount is Rs. 200."); return; }
            if(!tid || tid.length < 11 || tid.length > 12) { triggerToastDisplay("Enter valid 11-12 digit Transaction ID."); return; }


            db.collection("deposits").add({
                userId: currentUserId, amount: inputVal, gatewayChannel: activeGatewayChannel,
                transactionId: tid, status: "pending", timestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
            }).then(() => {
                closeModal('depositModal'); triggerToastDisplay("Deposit request submitted!");
                document.getElementById('pkrDepositInput').value = ""; document.getElementById('depositTxIDInput').value = "";
            });
        };


        window.executeP2PInternalTransfer = function() {
            const targetUserField = document.getElementById('transferTargetUsername').value.trim().toLowerCase();
            const amountField = parseFloat(document.getElementById('transferAmount').value);


            if(!targetUserField || isNaN(amountField) || amountField <= 0) { triggerToastDisplay("Please enter target username and valid amount."); return; }
            if(amountField > userWithdrawableBalance) { triggerToastDisplay("Insufficient Withdrawable Cash (Bonus balance cannot be transferred)."); return; }


            db.collection("users").where("username", "==", targetUserField).get().then(snapshot => {
                if(snapshot.empty) { throw new Error("User username not found."); }
                const targetDocNode = snapshot.docs[0];
                const destUid = targetDocNode.id;


                if(destUid === currentUserId) { throw new Error("You cannot transfer balance to yourself."); }


                userWithdrawableBalance -= amountField;
                updateWalletUI();


                const operationalBatch = db.batch();
                operationalBatch.update(db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId), {
                    withdrawableBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(-amountField),
                    balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(-amountField)
                });
                operationalBatch.update(db.collection("users").doc(destUid), {
                    withdrawableBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(amountField),
                    balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(amountField)
                });
                
                const sendLogRef = db.collection("deposits").doc();
                operationalBatch.set(sendLogRef, {
                    userId: currentUserId, amount: amountField, transactionId: "TRF-OUT-" + Math.floor(Math.random()*100000),
                    gatewayChannel: `Transfer Sent to ${targetUserField}`, status: "approved", timestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                });


                const recvLogRef = db.collection("deposits").doc();
                operationalBatch.set(recvLogRef, {
                    userId: destUid, amount: amountField, transactionId: "TRF-IN-" + Math.floor(Math.random()*100000),
                    gatewayChannel: `Transfer Received from ${userCustomUsernameToken}`, status: "approved", timestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                });


                return operationalBatch.commit();
            }).then(() => {
                closeModal('transferModal');
                triggerToastDisplay(`Sent Rs. ${amountField} directly to ${targetUserField}!`);
            }).catch(err => triggerToastDisplay(err.message || err));
        };


        function isWeekendFreeWithdrawal() {
            const day = new Date().getDay();
            return day === 0 || day === 6;
        }


        window.toggleBankNameField = function() {
            const method = document.getElementById("withdrawMethodSelect").value;
            document.getElementById("withdrawBankName").style.display = method === "Bank Transfer" ? "block" : "none";
        };


        window.calculateWithdrawTaxPreview = function() {
            const amt = parseFloat(document.getElementById("withdrawAmount").value) || 0;
            const isWeekend = isWeekendFreeWithdrawal();
            const taxPct = isWeekend ? 0 : (systemSettings.weekdayTaxPercent || 10);
            const taxAmt = (amt * taxPct) / 100;
            const netAmt = Math.max(0, amt - taxAmt);


            document.getElementById("withdrawDayLabel").innerText = isWeekend ? "Weekend (Sat/Sun)" : "Mon–Fri";
            document.getElementById("withdrawTaxRateLbl").innerText = isWeekend ? "0% FREE!" : `${taxPct}% (Rs. ${taxAmt.toFixed(2)})`;
            document.getElementById("withdrawTaxRateLbl").style.color = isWeekend ? "#10b981" : "#eab308";
            document.getElementById("withdrawNetPayoutLbl").innerText = `Rs. ${netAmt.toFixed(2)}`;
        };


        window.openWithdrawModal = function() {
            document.getElementById("modalWithdrawableBal").innerText = userWithdrawableBalance.toLocaleString("en-US", { minimumFractionDigits: 2 });
            calculateWithdrawTaxPreview();
            openModal('withdrawModal');
        };


        window.dispatchWithdrawalToCloud = function() {
            const method = document.getElementById("withdrawMethodSelect").value;
            const bankName = document.getElementById("withdrawBankName").value.trim();
            const title = document.getElementById('withdrawTitle').value.trim();
            const targetNo = document.getElementById('withdrawNumber').value.trim();
            const amt = parseFloat(document.getElementById('withdrawAmount').value);


            if (method === "Bank Transfer" && !bankName) { triggerToastDisplay("Please enter your Bank Name."); return; }
            if (!title || !targetNo || isNaN(amt)) { triggerToastDisplay("Please fill all withdrawal fields."); return; }
            if (amt < 100) { triggerToastDisplay("Minimum withdrawal limit is 100 PKR."); return; }
            if (amt > userWithdrawableBalance) { triggerToastDisplay("Insufficient Withdrawable Cash!"); return; }


            const isWeekend = isWeekendFreeWithdrawal();
            const taxPct = isWeekend ? 0 : (systemSettings.weekdayTaxPercent || 10);
            const taxAmt = (amt * taxPct) / 100;
            const netPayout = amt - taxAmt;
            const fullMethodLabel = method === "Bank Transfer" ? `Bank (${bankName})` : method;


            userWithdrawableBalance -= amt;
            updateWalletUI();


            db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId).update({
                withdrawableBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(-amt),
                balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(-amt)
            }).then(() => {
                return db.collection("withdrawals").add({
                    userId: currentUserId,
                    username: userCustomUsernameToken,
                    method: fullMethodLabel,
                    title: title,
                    targetNo: targetNo,
                    amount: amt,
                    taxPercent: taxPct,
                    netPayout: netPayout,
                    status: "pending",
                    timestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
                });
            }).then(() => { 
                closeModal('withdrawModal'); 
                triggerToastDisplay(`Withdrawal of Rs. ${amt} submitted! Net payout: Rs. ${netPayout.toFixed(2)}`); 
            });
        };

        window.pushInAppNotification = function(title, message, category = "system") {
            if (!currentUserId) return;
            db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId).collection("notifications").add({
                title: title,
                message: message,
                category: category,
                read: false,
                createdAt: Date.now()
            }).catch(() => {});
        };


        function listenToUserNotifications(uid) {
            db.collection("users").doc(uid).collection("notifications")
                .orderBy("createdAt", "desc").limit(30)
                .onSnapshot(snap => {
                    let unread = 0;
                    let html = "";
                    snap.forEach(doc => {
                        const n = doc.data();
                        if (!n.read) unread++;
                        html += `
                            <div onclick="markNotificationRead('${doc.id}')" style="padding:10px 12px; border-radius:12px; margin-bottom:8px; cursor:pointer; background:${n.read ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.03)' : 'rgba(14,165,233,0.14)'}; border:1px solid var(--border-color);">
                                <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
                                    <b style="font-size:12px; color:var(--text-main);">${n.title || "Notification"}</b>
                                    <span style="font-size:9px; color:${n.read ? 'var(--text-muted)' : '#38bdf8'}; font-weight:800;">${n.read ? "READ" : "NEW"}</span>
                                </div>
                                <p style="font-size:11px; color:var(--text-muted); margin-top:4px;">${n.message || ""}</p>
                            </div>`;
                    });
                    const badge = document.getElementById("notifUnreadBadge");
                    if (badge) {
                        badge.innerText = String(unread);
                        badge.style.display = unread > 0 ? "inline-flex" : "none";
                    }
                    const listEl = document.getElementById("notificationsInboxList");
                    if (listEl) listEl.innerHTML = html || "<p style='font-size:12px; color:var(--text-muted);'>No notifications yet.</p>";
                }, () => {});
        }


        window.markNotificationRead = function(notifId) {
            if (!currentUserId || !notifId) return;
            db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId).collection("notifications").doc(notifId).update({ read: true }).catch(() => {});
        };


        window.markAllNotificationsRead = function() {
            if (!currentUserId) return;
            db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId).collection("notifications").where("read", "==", false).get().then(snap => {
                const batch = db.batch();
                snap.forEach(doc => batch.update(doc.ref, { read: true }));
                return batch.commit();
            }).then(() => triggerToastDisplay("All notifications marked as read."));
        };


        window.openNotificationsInboxModal = function() {
            openModal("notificationsInboxModal");
        };


        window.redeemGiftCodeUser = function() {
            if (!currentUserId) { triggerToastDisplay("Please sign in first."); return; }
            let code = document.getElementById('userGiftCodeInput').value.trim().toUpperCase();
            if (!code) { triggerToastDisplay("Please enter gift code."); return; }


            const codeRef = db.collection("gift_codes").doc(code);
            const userRef = db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId);


            db.runTransaction(async (tx) => {
                const doc = await tx.get(codeRef);
                if (!doc.exists) throw new Error("INVALID_CODE");
                const d = doc.data();
                if (d.active === false) throw new Error("INACTIVE_CODE");


                const redeemedBy = d.redeemedBy || {};
                if (redeemedBy[currentUserId]) throw new Error("ALREADY_CLAIMED_BY_USER");


                const count = Number(d.redeemedCount || Object.keys(redeemedBy).length || 0);
                const maxRedemptions = Number(d.maxRedemptions || 0);
                if (maxRedemptions > 0 && count >= maxRedemptions) throw new Error("LIMIT_REACHED");


                const bonusAmt = Number(d.amount || 0);
                tx.update(codeRef, {
                    [`redeemedBy.${currentUserId}`]: Date.now(),
                    redeemedCount: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(1),
                    isRedeemed: true,
                    redeemedByUser: currentUserId
                });
                tx.update(userRef, {
                    bonusBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(bonusAmt),
                    balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(bonusAmt)
                });
                return bonusAmt;
            }).then((bonusAmt) => {
                triggerToastDisplay(`Gift Code Claimed! +Rs. ${bonusAmt} Play Bonus Added.`);
                document.getElementById('userGiftCodeInput').value = "";
                pushInAppNotification("Promo Code Claimed", `Added +Rs. ${bonusAmt} Play Bonus using code ${code}.`, "promo");
            }).catch((err) => {
                const msgs = {
                    INVALID_CODE: "Invalid gift code.",
                    INACTIVE_CODE: "This promo code is disabled.",
                    ALREADY_CLAIMED_BY_USER: "Aap pehle hi yeh promo code apne account par claim kar chuke hain.",
                    LIMIT_REACHED: "Is promo code ki total user limit poori ho chuki hai."
                };
                triggerToastDisplay(msgs[err.message] || "Could not redeem gift code.");
            });
        };


        window.triggerLuckyCoreSpin = function() {
            db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId).get().then(doc => {
                if(doc.data().hasSpunToday) { triggerToastDisplay("You already spun today! Come back tomorrow."); return; }
                
                const matrixPrizes = [5, 10, 4, 0, 10, 15, 20, 0, 12, 18];
                const winToken = matrixPrizes[Math.floor(Math.random() * matrixPrizes.length)];
                const wheel = document.getElementById('luckyBonusWheel');
                
                wheel.style.transform = "rotate(1440deg)";
                setTimeout(() => {
                    db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId).update({
                        bonusBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(winToken),
                        balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(winToken),
                        hasSpunToday: true
                    }).then(() => {
                        triggerToastDisplay(`Won +Rs. ${winToken} Play Bonus! Use on Nodes or Games.`);
                        wheel.style.transform = "rotate(0deg)";
                    });
                }, 3000);
            });
        };


        /* =========================================================================
           HISTORY, SUPPORT TICKETS & PROFILE
           ========================================================================= */
        function syncUserLedgerTracks() {
            db.collection("deposits").where("userId", "==", currentUserId).onSnapshot(depSnap => {
                db.collection("withdrawals").where("userId", "==", currentUserId).onSnapshot(withSnap => {
                    cachedUserHistoryLogs = [];
                    depSnap.forEach(doc => {
                        const d = doc.data();
                        cachedUserHistoryLogs.push({
                            type: "credit", title: `${d.gatewayChannel} Credit`, sub: `TID: ${d.transactionId}`,
                            amount: d.amount, status: d.status, ts: d.timestamp ? d.timestamp.toDate().getTime() : 0
                        });
                    });
                    withSnap.forEach(doc => {
                        const w = doc.data();
                        cachedUserHistoryLogs.push({
                            type: "debit", title: `${w.method || "Cash Out"} (${w.targetNo})`, sub: `${w.title} | Net: Rs.${(w.netPayout || w.amount).toFixed(0)}`,
                            amount: w.amount, status: w.status, ts: w.timestamp ? w.timestamp.toDate().getTime() : 0
                        });
                    });


                    cachedUserHistoryLogs.sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts);
                    renderFilteredHistoryLogs(cachedUserHistoryLogs);
                });
            });
        }


        function renderFilteredHistoryLogs(logsList) {
            let html = "";
            logsList.forEach(item => {
                let statusColor = item.status === 'approved' ? '#10b981' : (item.status === 'rejected' ? '#ef4444' : '#eab308');
                let amountSign = item.type === 'credit' ? '+' : '-';
                let amountColor = item.type === 'credit' ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444';
                
                html += `<div class="history-card">
                    <div>
                        <b style="font-size:13px; color:var(--text-main);">${item.title}</b>
                        <p style="font-size:10px; color:var(--text-muted); margin-top:2px;">${item.sub}</p>
                        <span style="font-size:9px; color:${statusColor}; font-weight:700; text-transform:uppercase;">${item.status}</span>
                    </div>
                    <b style="color:${amountColor}; font-size:13px;">${amountSign}Rs. ${item.amount.toLocaleString()}</b>
                </div>`;
            });
            document.getElementById('userHistoryRecordsList').innerHTML = html || "<p style='color:var(--text-muted); font-size:12px; text-align:center;'>No transaction history found.</p>";
        }


        window.submitSupportTicketUser = function() {
            let subj = document.getElementById('ticketSubjectInput').value.trim();
            let msg = document.getElementById('ticketMessageInput').value.trim();
            if(!subj || !msg) { triggerToastDisplay("Please enter subject and message."); return; }


            db.collection("support_tickets").add({
                userId: currentUserId, username: userCustomUsernameToken, subject: subj, message: msg,
                adminReply: "", status: "open", timestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
            }).then(() => {
                triggerToastDisplay("Support Ticket Submitted!");
                document.getElementById('ticketSubjectInput').value = "";
                document.getElementById('ticketMessageInput').value = "";
            });
        };


        function syncUserSupportTickets() {
            db.collection("support_tickets").where("userId", "==", currentUserId).onSnapshot(snap => {
                let html = "";
                snap.forEach(doc => {
                    let t = doc.data();
                    let badgeColor = t.status === "resolved" ? "#10b981" : "#eab308";
                    html += `<div class="info-card" style="padding:14px; margin-bottom:10px;">
                        <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
                            <b style="font-size:13px; color:var(--text-main);">${t.subject}</b>
                            <span style="font-size:9px; color:${badgeColor}; font-weight:800; text-transform:uppercase;">${t.status}</span>
                        </div>
                        <p style="font-size:11px; color:var(--text-muted); margin-top:4px;">${t.message}</p>
                        ${t.adminReply ? `<div style="background:rgba(14,165,233,0.12); padding:8px; border-radius:8px; margin-top:8px; font-size:11px; color:var(--sky-primary);"><b>Admin Reply:</b> ${t.adminReply}</div>` : ""}
                    </div>`;
                });
                document.getElementById('userSupportTicketsList').innerHTML = html || "<p style='color:var(--text-muted); font-size:11px;'>No support tickets created.</p>";
            });
        }


        window.openProfileEditModal = function() {
            document.getElementById('editUsernameInput').value = userCustomUsernameToken;
            if(lastUsernameChangeDate) {
                const daysSinceLastChange = (new Date() - lastUsernameChangeDate) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24);
                if(daysSinceLastChange < 30) {
                    const remainingDays = Math.ceil(30 - daysSinceLastChange);
                    document.getElementById('editUsernameInput').disabled = true;
                    document.getElementById('lblUsernameChangeNotice').innerText = `Username change locked. Available in ${remainingDays} days.`;
                } else {
                    document.getElementById('editUsernameInput').disabled = false;
                }
            }
            openModal('profileEditModal');
        };


        window.triggerProfilePictureFileInput = function() {
            document.getElementById('profileImageFileInput').click();
        };


        window.handleProfileImageSelect = function(event) {
            const file = event.target.files[0];
            if(!file) return;
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = function(e) {
                const img = new Image();
                img.onload = function() {
                    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
                    canvas.width = 120; canvas.height = 120;
                    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
                    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, 120, 120);
                    userProfilePicBase64 = canvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.7);
                    document.getElementById('modalProfilePreviewImg').src = userProfilePicBase64;
                };
                img.src = e.target.result;
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        };


        window.saveUserProfileChanges = function() {
            const newUsername = document.getElementById('editUsernameInput').value.trim().toLowerCase();
            let updates = {};
            if(userProfilePicBase64) updates.profilePic = userProfilePicBase64;


            if(newUsername && newUsername !== userCustomUsernameToken) {
                if(lastUsernameChangeDate && (new Date() - lastUsernameChangeDate) < (30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)) {
                    triggerToastDisplay("Username can only be changed once every 30 days."); return;
                }
                db.collection("users").where("username", "==", newUsername).get().then(snap => {
                    if(!snap.empty) { triggerToastDisplay("Username is already taken."); return; }
                    updates.username = newUsername;
                    updates.lastUsernameChangeDate = firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
                    db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId).update(updates).then(() => {
                        closeModal('profileEditModal'); triggerToastDisplay("Profile updated successfully!");
                    });
                });
            } else {
                if(Object.keys(updates).length > 0) {
                    db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId).update(updates).then(() => {
                        closeModal('profileEditModal'); triggerToastDisplay("Profile photo updated!");
                    });
                } else { closeModal('profileEditModal'); }
            }
        };


        /* =========================================================================
           SUPER ADMIN PANEL FUNCTIONS & SYSTEM CONTROLS
           ========================================================================= */
        function loadSystemGameAndTaxSettings() {
            db.collection("system_settings").doc("game_tax_config").onSnapshot(doc => {
                if (doc.exists) {
                    const d = doc.data();
                    if (d.aviatorDifficulty) systemSettings.aviatorDifficulty = d.aviatorDifficulty;
                    systemSettings.forcedCrashPoint = d.forcedCrashPoint || null;
                    if (d.weekdayTaxPercent !== undefined) systemSettings.weekdayTaxPercent = Number(d.weekdayTaxPercent);
                    if (d.crowdBaseCount !== undefined) systemSettings.crowdBaseCount = Number(d.crowdBaseCount);
                    systemSettings.maintenanceMode = Boolean(d.maintenanceMode);


                    const maintEl = document.getElementById("maintenanceOverlay");
                    if (maintEl) {
                        maintEl.style.display = systemSettings.maintenanceMode ? "flex" : "none";
                    }


                    const diffSel = document.getElementById("adminAviatorDifficulty");
                    const taxInp = document.getElementById("adminWithdrawTaxPct");
                    const crowdInp = document.getElementById("adminCrowdBaseInput");
                    const maintToggle = document.getElementById("adminMaintenanceToggle");
                    if (diffSel) diffSel.value = systemSettings.aviatorDifficulty;
                    if (taxInp) taxInp.value = systemSettings.weekdayTaxPercent;
                    if (crowdInp) crowdInp.value = systemSettings.crowdBaseCount;
                    if (maintToggle) maintToggle.checked = systemSettings.maintenanceMode;
                }
            });
        }


        window.saveSystemGameAndTaxSettings = function() {
            const diff = document.getElementById("adminAviatorDifficulty").value;
            const forcedCrash = parseFloat(document.getElementById("adminForceCrashInput").value);
            const taxPct = parseFloat(document.getElementById("adminWithdrawTaxPct").value);
            const crowdBase = parseInt(document.getElementById("adminCrowdBaseInput").value);
            const maintOn = document.getElementById("adminMaintenanceToggle").checked;


            const dtWinRate = parseFloat(document.getElementById("adminDtWinRateInput")?.value);
            const abWinRate = parseFloat(document.getElementById("adminAbWinRateInput")?.value);
            const minesEdge = parseFloat(document.getElementById("adminMinesEdgeInput")?.value);


            const payload = {
                aviatorDifficulty: diff,
                forcedCrashPoint: (!isNaN(forcedCrash) && forcedCrash >= 1.00) ? forcedCrash : null,
                weekdayTaxPercent: (!isNaN(taxPct) && taxPct >= 0) ? taxPct : 10,
                crowdBaseCount: (!isNaN(crowdBase) && crowdBase >= 50) ? crowdBase : 280,
                dtPlayerWinPct: (!isNaN(dtWinRate) && dtWinRate >= 5 && dtWinRate <= 85) ? dtWinRate : 45,
                abPlayerWinPct: (!isNaN(abWinRate) && abWinRate >= 5 && abWinRate <= 85) ? abWinRate : 46,
                minesHouseEdgePct: (!isNaN(minesEdge) && minesEdge >= 1 && minesEdge <= 40) ? minesEdge : 4,
                maintenanceMode: maintOn
            };
            const maxCrashCap = parseFloat(document.getElementById("adminMaxCrashCapInput")?.value);
            const minMinesInp = parseInt(document.getElementById("adminMinMinesInput")?.value, 10);
            const forceMineBomb = Boolean(document.getElementById("adminForceNextMineBomb")?.checked);
            payload.maxCrashCap = (!isNaN(maxCrashCap) && maxCrashCap >= 2) ? maxCrashCap : 500;
            payload.minMinesAllowed = (!isNaN(minMinesInp) && minMinesInp >= 4 && minMinesInp <= 24) ? minMinesInp : 4;
            payload.forceNextMineBomb = forceMineBomb;
            payload.forceDtWinner = document.getElementById("adminForceDtWinner")?.value || "AUTO";
            payload.forceAbWinner = document.getElementById("adminForceAbWinner")?.value || "AUTO";
            systemSettings.forceDtWinner = payload.forceDtWinner;
            systemSettings.forceAbWinner = payload.forceAbWinner;
            payload.gamesEnabled = {
                aviator: document.getElementById("adminEnableAviatorToggle")?.checked ?? true,
                dragonTiger: document.getElementById("adminEnableDtToggle")?.checked ?? true,
                andarBahar: document.getElementById("adminEnableAbToggle")?.checked ?? true,
                mines: document.getElementById("adminEnableMinesToggle")?.checked ?? true
            };
            systemSettings.minMinesAllowed = payload.minMinesAllowed;
            systemSettings.maxCrashCap = payload.maxCrashCap;
            systemSettings.forceNextMineBomb = payload.forceNextMineBomb;
            systemSettings.dtPlayerWinPct = payload.dtPlayerWinPct;
            systemSettings.abPlayerWinPct = payload.abPlayerWinPct;
            systemSettings.minesHouseEdgePct = payload.minesHouseEdgePct;
            systemSettings.gamesEnabled = payload.gamesEnabled;


            applyAdminGameControlsUI();
            db.collection("system_settings").doc("game_tax_config").set(payload, { merge: true }).then(() => {
                document.getElementById("adminForceCrashInput").value = "";
                triggerToastDisplay("System, Aviator & Maintenance Settings Saved!");
            });
        };


        function initializeRealtimeBroadcastListener() {
            db.collection("broadcasts").orderBy("timestamp", "desc").limit(1).onSnapshot(snapshot => {
                if(!snapshot.empty) {
                    const data = snapshot.docs[0].data();
                    document.getElementById('tickerBroadcastTitle').innerText = data.title;
                    document.getElementById('tickerBroadcastMsg').innerText = data.message;
                    document.getElementById('liveBroadcastTickerContainer').style.display = 'flex';
                }
            });
        }

            const msgs = [

                "hamza_pk won Rs. 14,500 on Aviator (14.5x)!",
                "0315****19 activated Rig Stratum-1 (+Rs. 380/Day)!",

                "0345****91 withdrew Rs. 8,500 0% Tax-Free!"
            ];
            let idx = 0;
            const homeEl = document.getElementById('liveSocialProofText');
            const authEl = document.getElementById('authLiveTickerMsg');
            if(homeEl) homeEl.innerText = msgs[0];
            if(authEl) authEl.innerText = msgs[0];


            setInterval(() => {
                idx = (idx + 1) % msgs.length;
                if(homeEl) homeEl.innerText = msgs[idx];
                if(authEl) authEl.innerText = msgs[idx];
            }, 4000);
        }

                loadAdminLiveTracker();
