        window.switchAdminSubView = function(tab) {
            const sections = {
                tracker: ['adminTabTracker', 'adminTrackerSection'],
                deposits: ['adminTabDep', 'adminDepositsSection'],
                users: ['adminTabUsers', 'adminUsersSection'],
                nodes: ['adminTabNodes', 'adminNodesSection'],
                gameControl: ['adminTabGameControl', 'adminGameControlSection'],
                tickets: ['adminTabTickets', 'adminTicketsSection'],
                payments: ['adminTabPayments', 'adminPaymentsSection'],
                broadcast: ['adminTabBroadcast', 'adminBroadcastSection'],
                analytics: ['adminTabAnalytics', 'adminAnalyticsSection']
            };
            Object.keys(sections).forEach(key => {
                document.getElementById(sections[key][0]).classList.toggle('active', key === tab);
                document.getElementById(sections[key][1]).style.display = key === tab ? 'block' : 'none';
            });
        };


        function loadAdminLiveTracker() {
            db.collection("presence").orderBy("lastActive", "desc").onSnapshot(snap => {
                let html = "";
                const now = new Date();
                snap.forEach(doc => {
                    let p = doc.data();
                    let lastTime = p.lastActive ? p.lastActive.toDate() : null;
                    let isOnline = lastTime && (now - lastTime) < 120000;
                    if(isOnline) {
                        html += `<div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.03); padding:10px; border-radius:10px; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
                            <div><b>${p.username}</b> <span style="font-size:10px; color:#10b981;">● Online</span><br><span style="font-size:10px; color:#94a3b8;">Viewing: ${p.currentPage}</span></div>
                            <span style="font-size:10px; color:#38bdf8;">${lastTime.toLocaleTimeString()}</span>
                        </div>`;
                    }
                });
                document.getElementById('adminLiveTrackerFeed').innerHTML = html || "<p style='color:#94a3b8;'>No users currently active online.</p>";
            });
        }


        function loadAdminTransactionsList() {
            db.collection("deposits").where("status", "==", "pending").onSnapshot(snap => {
                let html = "";
                snap.forEach(doc => {
                    let d = doc.data();
                    html += `<div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.03); padding:10px; border-radius:10px; border-left:3px solid #eab308;">
                        <b>Deposit: Rs. ${d.amount} (${d.gatewayChannel})</b><br>
                        <span style="font-size:11px; color:#94a3b8;">TID: ${d.transactionId}</span>
                        <div style="display:flex; gap:6px; margin-top:6px;">
                            <button class="buy-plan-btn" onclick="approveDepositReq('${doc.id}', '${d.userId}', ${d.amount})" style="padding:4px 10px; font-size:11px; background:#10b981;">Approve</button>
                            <button class="buy-plan-btn" onclick="rejectTransactionReq('deposits', '${doc.id}')" style="padding:4px 10px; font-size:11px; background:#ef4444;">Reject</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;
                });
                document.getElementById('adminPendingDepositsTrack').innerHTML = html || "<p style='color:#94a3b8;'>No pending deposits.</p>";
            });


            db.collection("withdrawals").where("status", "==", "pending").onSnapshot(snap => {
                let html = "";
                snap.forEach(doc => {
                    let w = doc.data();
                    const netPay = w.netPayout !== undefined ? w.netPayout : w.amount;
                    html += `<div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.03); padding:10px; border-radius:10px; border-left:3px solid #ef4444;">
                        <b>Withdraw: Rs. ${w.amount} | Net Pay: Rs. ${netPay.toFixed(0)}</b><br>
                        <span style="font-size:11px; color:#38bdf8;">${w.method || 'Account'} — ${w.title} (${w.targetNo})</span>
                        <div style="display:flex; gap:6px; margin-top:6px;">
                            <button class="buy-plan-btn" onclick="approveWithdrawReq('${doc.id}')" style="padding:4px 10px; font-size:11px; background:#0ea5e9;">Mark Paid</button>
                            <button class="buy-plan-btn" onclick="rejectWithdrawReq('${doc.id}', '${w.userId}', ${w.amount})" style="padding:4px 10px; font-size:11px; background:#ef4444;">Reject & Refund</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;
                });
                document.getElementById('adminPendingWithdrawalsTrack').innerHTML = html || "<p style='color:#94a3b8;'>No pending withdrawals.</p>";
            });
        }


        window.approveDepositReq = function(docId, userId, amount) {
            db.collection("users").doc(userId).get().then(doc => {
                let hasDepositedBefore = doc.exists ? (doc.data().hasDepositedBefore || false) : false;
                let bonusRate = hasDepositedBefore ? 0.04 : 0.08;
                let bonusAmount = amount * bonusRate;


                const batch = db.batch();
                batch.update(db.collection("deposits").doc(docId), { status: "approved" });
                batch.update(db.collection("users").doc(userId), {
                    withdrawableBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(amount),
                    bonusBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(bonusAmount),
                    balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(amount + bonusAmount),
                    hasDepositedBefore: true
                });


                batch.commit().then(() => {
                    processMultiTierReferralBonus(userId, amount);
                    triggerToastDisplay(`Deposit Approved! Rs.${amount} Cash + Rs.${bonusAmount} Play Bonus.`);
                });
            });
        };


        window.rejectTransactionReq = function(col, docId) {
            db.collection(col).doc(docId).update({ status: "rejected" }).then(() => triggerToastDisplay("Request Rejected."));
        };


        window.approveWithdrawReq = function(docId) {
            db.collection("withdrawals").doc(docId).update({ status: "approved" }).then(() => triggerToastDisplay("Withdrawal Marked Paid!"));
        };


        window.rejectWithdrawReq = function(docId, userId, amt) {
            const batch = db.batch();
            batch.update(db.collection("withdrawals").doc(docId), { status: "rejected" });
            batch.update(db.collection("users").doc(userId), {
                withdrawableBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(amt),
                balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(amt)
            });
            batch.commit().then(() => triggerToastDisplay("Withdrawal Rejected & Refunded to Withdrawable Cash!"));
        };


        function loadAdminUsersList() {
            db.collection("users").onSnapshot(snap => {
                allAdminUsersCache = [];
                snap.forEach(doc => allAdminUsersCache.push({ id: doc.id, data: doc.data() }));
                if (document.getElementById("adminUserSearchInput")?.value) { renderAdminUsersFiltered(); return; }
                let html = "";
                snap.forEach(doc => {
                    let u = doc.data();
                    let blockText = u.isBlocked ? "Unblock" : "Block";
                    let blockColor = u.isBlocked ? "#10b981" : "#ef4444";
                    let wBal = u.withdrawableBalance !== undefined ? u.withdrawableBalance : (u.balance || 0);
                    let bBal = u.bonusBalance || 0;
                    html += `<div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.03); padding:10px; border-radius:10px; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
                        <div>
                            <b>${u.username}</b> <span style="font-size:10px; color:#94a3b8;">(${u.email})</span><br>
                            <span style="font-size:10px; color:#10b981;">Cash: Rs.${wBal}</span> | <span style="font-size:10px; color:#eab308;">Bonus: Rs.${bBal}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div style="display:flex; gap:5px;">
                            <button class="buy-plan-btn" onclick="adminAddBalanceToUser('${doc.id}', '${u.username}')" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:10px; background:#eab308; color:#000;">+Cash</button>
                            <button class="buy-plan-btn" onclick="adminToggleBlockUser('${doc.id}', ${u.isBlocked})" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:10px; background:${blockColor};">${blockText}</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;
                });
                document.getElementById('adminUsersTrack').innerHTML = html;
            });
        }


        window.adminAddBalanceToUser = function(uid, uname) {
            let addAmt = parseFloat(prompt(`Enter Withdrawable Cash PKR amount to add to ${uname}:`));
            if(!isNaN(addAmt) && addAmt > 0) {
                db.collection("users").doc(uid).update({
                    withdrawableBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(addAmt),
                    balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(addAmt)
                }).then(() => triggerToastDisplay(`Added Rs. ${addAmt} Cash to ${uname}!`));
            }
        };


        window.adminToggleBlockUser = function(uid, currentStatus) {
            db.collection("users").doc(uid).update({ isBlocked: !currentStatus }).then(() => triggerToastDisplay(currentStatus ? "User Unblocked!" : "User Blocked!"));
        };


        window.createCustomBonusCode = function() {
            let code = document.getElementById('adminGiftCode').value.trim().toUpperCase();
            let amt = parseFloat(document.getElementById('adminGiftAmount').value);
            if(!code || isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) { triggerToastDisplay("Enter valid code name and amount."); return; }


            db.collection("gift_codes").doc(code).set({
                code: code,
                amount: amt,
                maxRedemptions: 500,
                redeemedBy: {},
                redeemedCount: 0,
                active: true,
                isRedeemed: false,
                timestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
            }, { merge: true }).then(() => {
                triggerToastDisplay(`Multi-User Gift Code ${code} (Rs. ${amt}, 1x per user) Created!`);
                document.getElementById('adminGiftCode').value = "";
                document.getElementById('adminGiftAmount').value = "";
            });
        };


        /* ADMIN NODES MANAGER (ADD, EDIT, DELETE) */
        function renderAdminNodesList() {
            const listEl = document.getElementById('adminExistingNodesList');
            if (!listEl) return;
            let html = "";
            Object.values(activeNodesMap).forEach(n => {
                html += `<div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.04); padding:8px 10px; border-radius:10px; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
                    <div>
                        <b style="color:#fff;">${n.nodeId}</b> <span style="font-size:9px; color:#38bdf8;">[${n.category.toUpperCase()}]</span><br>
                        <span style="color:#94a3b8;">Cost: Rs.${n.cost} | Daily: Rs.${n.dailyYield} | ${n.duration}d</span>
                    </div>
                    <div style="display:flex; gap:5px;">
                        <button class="buy-plan-btn" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:10px;" onclick="populateAdminNodeEditor('${n.nodeId.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">Edit</button>
                        <button class="buy-plan-btn" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:10px; background:#ef4444;" onclick="deleteNodeConfigOverride('${n.nodeId.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">Del</button>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            });
            listEl.innerHTML = html || "<p style='color:#94a3b8;'>No nodes configured.</p>";
        }


        window.populateAdminNodeEditor = function(nodeId) {
            const n = activeNodesMap[nodeId];
            if (!n) return;
            document.getElementById('editNodeCategory').value = n.category;
            document.getElementById('editNodeName').value = n.nodeId;
            document.getElementById('editNodePrice').value = n.cost;
            document.getElementById('editNodeDaily').value = n.dailyYield;
            document.getElementById('editNodeDuration').value = n.duration;
            document.getElementById('editNodeImg').value = n.imageUrl;
            triggerToastDisplay(`Loaded ${n.nodeId} into editor!`);
        };


        window.saveNodeConfigOverride = function() {
            let category = document.getElementById('editNodeCategory').value;
            let nodeId = document.getElementById('editNodeName').value.trim();
            let cost = parseFloat(document.getElementById('editNodePrice').value);
            let dailyYield = parseFloat(document.getElementById('editNodeDaily').value);
            let duration = parseInt(document.getElementById('editNodeDuration').value) || 30;
            let imgUrl = document.getElementById('editNodeImg').value.trim();


            if(!nodeId || isNaN(cost) || isNaN(dailyYield)) { triggerToastDisplay("Enter valid Node Name, Price, and Daily Yield."); return; }


            let payload = {
                nodeId: nodeId,
                category: category,
                cost: cost,
                dailyYield: dailyYield,
                duration: duration,
                isDeleted: false,
                updatedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
            };
            if(imgUrl) payload.imageUrl = imgUrl;


            db.collection("node_configs").doc(nodeId).set(payload, { merge: true }).then(() => {
                triggerToastDisplay(`Saved ${nodeId} successfully!`);
            });
        };


        window.deleteNodeConfigOverride = function(nodeId) {
            db.collection("node_configs").doc(nodeId).set({ nodeId: nodeId, isDeleted: true }, { merge: true }).then(() => {
                triggerToastDisplay(`Removed ${nodeId}`);
            });
        };


        function loadAdminSupportTicketsList() {
            db.collection("support_tickets").orderBy("timestamp", "desc").onSnapshot(snap => {
                let html = "";
                snap.forEach(doc => {
                    let t = doc.data();
                    html += `<div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.03); padding:10px; border-radius:10px; border-left:3px solid #38bdf8;">
                        <b>${t.subject}</b> <span style="font-size:10px; color:#94a3b8;">by ${t.username}</span><br>
                        <p style="font-size:11px; color:#fff; margin-top:4px;">${t.message}</p>
                        ${t.adminReply ? `<p style="font-size:10px; color:#10b981; margin-top:4px;">Replied: ${t.adminReply}</p>` : 
                        `<button class="buy-plan-btn" onclick="adminReplySupportTicket('${doc.id}')" style="margin-top:6px; padding:4px 10px; font-size:10px;">Reply & Resolve</button>`}
                    </div>`;
                });
                document.getElementById('adminTicketsListFeed').innerHTML = html || "<p style='color:#94a3b8;'>No support tickets.</p>";
            });
        }


        window.adminReplySupportTicket = function(ticketId) {
            let replyMsg = prompt("Enter reply message to user:");
            if(replyMsg) {
                db.collection("support_tickets").doc(ticketId).update({ adminReply: replyMsg, status: "resolved" }).then(() => triggerToastDisplay("Reply Sent!"));
            }
        };


        window.loadSelectedPaymentDetailsAdmin = function() {
            let method = document.getElementById('adminPaymentSelect').value;
            let currentConf = paymentGatewayMerchantConfig[method] || { title: "", number: "" };
            document.getElementById('adminPayTitle').value = currentConf.title;
            document.getElementById('adminPayNumber').value = currentConf.number;
        };


        window.updatePaymentMethodDetailsAdmin = function() {
            let method = document.getElementById('adminPaymentSelect').value;
            let title = document.getElementById('adminPayTitle').value.trim();
            let number = document.getElementById('adminPayNumber').value.trim();
            if(!title || !number) { triggerToastDisplay("Enter account title and number."); return; }


            let payload = {};
            payload[method] = { title: title, number: number };
            db.collection("system_settings").doc("payment_gateways").set(payload, { merge: true }).then(() => triggerToastDisplay(`${method} Payment Details Updated!`));
        };


        window.dispatchGlobalBroadcast = function() {
            let title = document.getElementById('broadcastTitleInput').value.trim();
            let msg = document.getElementById('broadcastMessageInput').value.trim();
            if(!title || !msg) { triggerToastDisplay("Enter announcement title and message."); return; }


            db.collection("broadcasts").add({ title: title, message: msg, timestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }).then(() => {
                triggerToastDisplay("Global Broadcast Sent!");
                document.getElementById('broadcastTitleInput').value = "";
                document.getElementById('broadcastMessageInput').value = "";
            });
        };


        function loadAdminAnalyticsReport() {
            db.collection("users").get().then(snap => {
                let totalUsers = snap.size;
                document.getElementById('repDAU').innerText = Math.max(1, Math.floor(totalUsers * 0.6));
                document.getElementById('repWAU').innerText = Math.max(1, Math.floor(totalUsers * 0.85));
                document.getElementById('repMAU').innerText = totalUsers;
            });


            db.collection("deposits").where("status", "==", "approved").get().then(snap => {
                let sum = 0;
                snap.forEach(doc => { if(!doc.data().gatewayChannel.includes("Profit")) sum += (doc.data().amount || 0); });
                document.getElementById('repTotalRevenue').innerText = "Rs. " + sum.toLocaleString();
            });
        }


        window.exportUserDataCSV = function() {
            db.collection("users").get().then(snap => {
                let csv = "UID,Username,Email,WithdrawableCash,BonusBalance,Miners,Sponsor\n";
                snap.forEach(doc => {
                    let u = doc.data();
                    csv += `${doc.id},${u.username},${u.email},${u.withdrawableBalance || 0},${u.bonusBalance || 0},${u.minersCount || 0},${u.referredBy || 'none'}\n`;
                });
                downloadCSVFile(csv, "VestifyPro_Users_Report.csv");
            });
        };


        window.exportTransactionDataCSV = function() {
            db.collection("deposits").get().then(snap => {
                let csv = "DocID,UserID,Amount,Channel,TransactionID,Status\n";
                snap.forEach(doc => {
                    let d = doc.data();
                    csv += `${doc.id},${d.userId},${d.amount},${d.gatewayChannel},${d.transactionId},${d.status}\n`;
                });
                downloadCSVFile(csv, "VestifyPro_Transactions_Report.csv");
            });
        };


        function downloadCSVFile(csvContent, filename) {
            const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const a = document.createElement('a');
            a.href = url; a.download = filename; a.click();
            triggerToastDisplay(`Downloaded ${filename}!`);
        }


        function initializeCountdownClockTicker() {
            setInterval(() => {
                const now = new Date();
                const midnight = new Date();
                midnight.setHours(24, 0, 0, 0);
                const diff = midnight - now;
                const h = Math.floor((diff / (1000 * 60 * 60)) % 24);
                const m = Math.floor((diff / 1000 / 60) % 60);
                const s = Math.floor((diff / 1000) % 60);
                document.getElementById('countdownTimerClock').innerText = 
                    `${String(h).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(m).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(s).padStart(2,'0')}`;
            }, 1000);
        }


        window.toggleFaqVisibility = function(el) {
            const ans = el.querySelector('.faq-a');
            ans.style.display = ans.style.display === 'block' ? 'none' : 'block';
        };


        window.routeToView = function(pageId, navId) {
            const ge = systemSettings.gamesEnabled || {};
            if (pageId === "pageAviator" && ge.aviator === false) {
                triggerToastDisplay("Aviator is temporarily paused by Admin.");
                return;
            }
            document.querySelectorAll('.app-view-page').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active-page'));
            document.getElementById(pageId).classList.add('active-page');
            document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n => n.classList.remove('active'));
            document.getElementById(navId).classList.add('active');
            updateLivePresenceActivity(pageId.replace('page', ''));
        };


        window.switchPlanCategory = function(gridId, tabId) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.plans-grid').forEach(g => g.classList.remove('active-grid'));
            document.getElementById(gridId).classList.add('active-grid');
            document.querySelectorAll('.plan-tab-trigger').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active-tab'));
            document.getElementById(tabId).classList.add('active-tab');
        };


        window.selectDepositGatewayChannel = function(method) {
            activeGatewayChannel = method;
            ['chkEasyPaisa', 'chkJazzCash', 'chkUSDT'].forEach(id => {
                const el = document.getElementById(id);
                if(el) el.style.borderColor = 'var(--border-color)';
            });
            const targetBtn = document.getElementById('chk' + method);
            if(targetBtn) targetBtn.style.borderColor = '#0ea5e9';
            
            const conf = paymentGatewayMerchantConfig[method] || { number: "03379827882", title: "Account" };
            document.getElementById('txtMerchantAccountNo').innerText = conf.number;
            document.getElementById('txtMerchantAccountTitle').innerText = conf.title;
        };


        window.openDepositModal = function() {
            selectDepositGatewayChannel(activeGatewayChannel);
            openModal('depositModal');
        };
        window.openTransferModal = () => openModal('transferModal');
        window.openModal = id => document.getElementById(id).classList.add('modal-open');
        window.closeModal = id => document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('modal-open');


        window.recalculateYieldProjection = function() {
            const budget = Math.max(0, parseFloat(document.getElementById("yieldCalcBudgetInput")?.value) || 0);
            const daily = Math.round(budget * 0.08);
            const monthly = daily * 30;
            const badge = document.getElementById("yieldCalcDailyBadge");
            const d30 = document.getElementById("yieldCalc30dVal");
            if (badge) badge.innerText = `+Rs. ${daily.toLocaleString()}/day`;
            if (d30) d30.innerText = `Rs. ${monthly.toLocaleString()}`;
        };


        window.toggleUserAutoReinvestPref = function() {
            const checked = Boolean(document.getElementById("userAutoReinvestToggle")?.checked);
            localStorage.setItem("vestify_auto_reinvest_alert", checked ? "1" : "0");
            triggerToastDisplay(checked ? "Auto-Reinvest Alert ON" : "Auto-Reinvest Alert OFF");
        };


        window.calculateReferralTierPreview = function() {
            const amt = Math.max(0, parseFloat(document.getElementById("refCalcDepositInput")?.value) || 0);
            const t1 = (amt * 0.10).toFixed(0);
            const t2 = (amt * 0.03).toFixed(0);
            const t3 = (amt * 0.01).toFixed(0);
            const el = document.getElementById("refCalcBreakdownText");
            if (el) el.innerText = `Tier 1: Rs. ${Number(t1).toLocaleString()} | Tier 2: Rs. ${Number(t2).toLocaleString()} | Tier 3: Rs. ${Number(t3).toLocaleString()}`;
        };


        window.applyBroadcastPreset = function(presetType) {
            const titleEl = document.getElementById("broadcastTitleInput");
            const msgEl = document.getElementById("broadcastMessageInput");
            if (!titleEl || !msgEl) return;
            if (presetType === "weekend") {
                titleEl.value = "Weekend 0% Tax-Free Payouts!";
                msgEl.value = "Saturday aur Sunday ko withdrawals par 0% tax hai. Apna cashout jaldi lagayein!";
            } else if (presetType === "promo") {
                titleEl.value = "Special Play Bonus Live!";
                msgEl.value = "Naya gift code apply karein aur Nodes ya Games mein extra play bonus hasil karein.";
            }
            triggerToastDisplay("Broadcast preset loaded.");
        };


        window.loadAdminGiftCodesList = function() {
            const feed = document.getElementById("adminGiftCodesListFeed");
            if (!feed) return;
            db.collection("gift_codes").get().then(snap => {
                if (snap.empty) {
                    feed.innerHTML = "<p style='color:#94a3b8;'>No gift codes found.</p>";
                    return;
                }
                let out = "";
                snap.forEach(doc => {
                    const g = doc.data();
                    const isActive = g.active !== false;
                    const used = Number(g.redeemedCount || 0);
                    out += `<div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.04); padding:8px 10px; border-radius:10px; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
                        <div>
                            <b>${doc.id}</b> — <span style="color:#10b981;">Rs. ${g.amount || 0}</span> <span style="color:#94a3b8;">(Used: ${used})</span><br>
                            <span style="font-size:10px; color:${isActive ? '#38bdf8' : '#ef4444'};">${isActive ? 'ACTIVE' : 'DISABLED'}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div style="display:flex; gap:5px;">
                            <button type="button" class="aviator-chip-btn" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:10px;" onclick="toggleGiftCodeStatus('${doc.id}', ${isActive})">${isActive ? 'Disable' : 'Enable'}</button>
                            <button type="button" class="aviator-chip-btn" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:10px; color:#ef4444;" onclick="deleteGiftCodeDoc('${doc.id}')">Del</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;
                });
                feed.innerHTML = out;
            }).catch(() => {
                feed.innerHTML = "<p style='color:#ef4444;'>Failed to load gift codes.</p>";
            });
        };


        window.toggleGiftCodeStatus = function(codeId, currentActive) {
            db.collection("gift_codes").doc(codeId).update({ active: !currentActive }).then(() => {
                triggerToastDisplay(`${codeId} updated.`);
                loadAdminGiftCodesList();
            });
        };


        window.deleteGiftCodeDoc = function(codeId) {
            db.collection("gift_codes").doc(codeId).delete().then(() => {
                triggerToastDisplay(`${codeId} deleted.`);
                loadAdminGiftCodesList();
            });
        };


        function applyAdminGameControlsUI() {
            const ge = systemSettings.gamesEnabled || { aviator: true, dragonTiger: true, andarBahar: true, mines: true };
            const nvAv = document.getElementById("nvAviator");
            if (nvAv) nvAv.style.opacity = ge.aviator === false ? "0.45" : "1";
            const tDT = document.getElementById("hubTabDT");
            const tAB = document.getElementById("hubTabAB");
            const tMN = document.getElementById("hubTabMines");
            if (tDT) tDT.style.opacity = ge.dragonTiger === false ? "0.45" : "1";
            if (tAB) tAB.style.opacity = ge.andarBahar === false ? "0.45" : "1";
            if (tMN) tMN.style.opacity = ge.mines === false ? "0.45" : "1";
        }


        function triggerToastDisplay(msg) {
            const t = document.getElementById('rewardToast');
            t.innerText = msg; t.classList.add('toast-show');
            setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('toast-show'), 3500);
        }
    </script>

    <!-- MODAL 6: SAVED PAYOUT ACCOUNT MODAL -->
    <div id="savedPayoutAccountModal" class="premium-modal">
        <div class="modal-content" style="text-align:left;">
            <div class="close-modal-btn" onclick="closeModal('savedPayoutAccountModal')">&times;</div>
            <h4 style="color:var(--sky-primary); text-align:center; margin-bottom:8px;"><i class="fa-solid fa-address-card"></i> Save Withdrawal Account</h4>
            <select id="savedAccMethodInput" class="modal-input" style="margin-top:6px;">
                <option value="EasyPaisa">EasyPaisa</option>
                <option value="JazzCash">JazzCash</option>
                <option value="Bank Transfer">Bank Transfer</option>
            </select>
            <input type="text" id="savedAccTitleInput" class="modal-input" placeholder="Account Holder Name">
            <input type="text" id="savedAccNumberInput" class="modal-input" placeholder="Account / Mobile / IBAN Number">
            <button type="button" class="buy-plan-btn" onclick="saveUserPayoutAccount()" style="width:100%; margin-top:12px; padding:11px;">Save Account</button>
        </div>
    </div>


<script>


        let allAdminUsersCache = [];
        let activeHistoryTypeFilter = "all";


        window.openSavedPayoutAccountModal = function() {
            openModal("savedPayoutAccountModal");
        };


        window.saveUserPayoutAccount = function() {
            const method = document.getElementById("savedAccMethodInput")?.value || "EasyPaisa";
            const title = document.getElementById("savedAccTitleInput")?.value.trim() || "";
            const number = document.getElementById("savedAccNumberInput")?.value.trim() || "";
            if (!title || !number) {
                triggerToastDisplay("Please enter account title and number.");
                return;
            }
            const accountObj = { method, title, number, updatedAt: Date.now() };
            localStorage.setItem("vestify_saved_payout_acc_" + (currentUserId || "guest"), JSON.stringify(accountObj));
            renderSavedPayoutAccountCard();
            closeModal("savedPayoutAccountModal");
            triggerToastDisplay("Payout account saved!");
        };


        window.applySavedPayoutAccountToWithdraw = function() {
            const raw = localStorage.getItem("vestify_saved_payout_acc_" + (currentUserId || "guest"));
            if (!raw) return;
            try {
                const acc = JSON.parse(raw);
                const sel = document.getElementById("withdrawMethodSelect");
                const titleInp = document.getElementById("withdrawTitle");
                const numInp = document.getElementById("withdrawNumber");
                const bankInp = document.getElementById("withdrawBankName");
                if (sel) {
                    if (acc.method.startsWith("Bank")) {
                        sel.value = "Bank Transfer";
                        if (bankInp) {
                            bankInp.style.display = "block";
                            bankInp.value = acc.method.replace(/^Bank\s*\(?|\)?$/g, "").trim();
                        }
                    } else {
                        sel.value = acc.method;
                        if (bankInp) bankInp.style.display = "none";
                    }
                }
                if (titleInp) titleInp.value = acc.title || "";
                if (numInp) numInp.value = acc.number || "";
                triggerToastDisplay("Saved account details filled.");
            } catch (e) {}
        };


        function renderSavedPayoutAccountCard() {
            const el = document.getElementById("savedPayoutAccountsList");
            if (!el) return;
            const raw = localStorage.getItem("vestify_saved_payout_acc_" + (currentUserId || "guest"));
            if (!raw) {
                el.innerHTML = "No saved payout accounts yet.";
                return;
            }
            try {
                const acc = JSON.parse(raw);
                el.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; background:var(--bg-sub); padding:10px; border-radius:12px; border:1px solid var(--border-color);">
                        <div>
                            <b style="color:var(--text-main);">${acc.method}</b> — <span style="color:var(--sky-primary);">${acc.number}</span><br>
                            <span style="font-size:10px; color:var(--text-muted);">Title: ${acc.title}</span>
                        </div>
                        <button type="button" class="aviator-chip-btn" onclick="openWithdrawModal(); setTimeout(applySavedPayoutAccountToWithdraw, 120);" style="padding:5px 10px; font-size:10px; color:#38bdf8;">Use</button>
                    </div>`;
            } catch (e) {
                el.innerHTML = "No saved payout accounts yet.";
            }
        }


        function updateDetailedLeasesTracker(snapshot) {
            const card = document.getElementById("userLeasesDetailedCard");
            const list = document.getElementById("userLeasesExpiryList");
            const badge = document.getElementById("leasesActiveCountBadge");
            if (!card || !list || !badge) return;


            if (!snapshot || snapshot.empty) {
                card.style.display = "none";
                return;
            }


            card.style.display = "block";
            badge.innerText = `${snapshot.size} Active`;
            let html = "";
            snapshot.forEach(doc => {
                const l = doc.data();
                const dur = Number(l.durationDays) || 30;
                const startMs = l.timestamp ? l.timestamp.toDate().getTime() : Date.now();
                const daysPassed = Math.floor((Date.now() - startMs) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
                const daysLeft = Math.max(0, dur - daysPassed);
                html += `
                    <div style="background:var(--bg-sub); border:1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius:12px; padding:9px 11px; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; font-size:11px;">
                        <div>
                            <b style="color:var(--text-main);">${l.planName || "Node"}</b>
                            <div style="font-size:10px; color:#10b981; margin-top:2px;">Daily: +Rs. ${(Number(l.dailyYield) || 0).toLocaleString()}/day</div>
                        </div>
                        <div style="text-align:right;">
                            <span style="font-weight:800; color:${daysLeft <= 3 ? '#ef4444' : '#38bdf8'};">${daysLeft}d left</span>
                            <div style="font-size:9px; color:var(--text-muted);">of ${dur}d</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>`;
            });
            list.innerHTML = html;
        }


        window.setHistoryCategoryFilter = function(cat) {
            activeHistoryTypeFilter = cat;
            ["all", "credit", "debit"].forEach(k => {
                const btn = document.getElementById("histFilter" + k.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + k.slice(1));
                if (btn) btn.classList.toggle("active", k === cat);
            });
            filterUserHistoryLogs();
        };


        window.filterUserHistoryLogs = function() {
            const query = (document.getElementById("historySearchInput")?.value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
            const filtered = cachedUserHistoryLogs.filter(item => {
                const typeOk = activeHistoryTypeFilter === "all" || item.type === activeHistoryTypeFilter;
                const textOk = !query ||
                    item.title.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
                    item.sub.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
                    item.status.toLowerCase().includes(query);
                return typeOk && textOk;
            });
            renderFilteredHistoryLogs(filtered);
        };


        function renderDirectReferralTeamList(itemsInput) {
            const box = document.getElementById("directReferralMembersList");
            if (!box) return;
            const list = Array.isArray(itemsInput)
                ? itemsInput.map(x => x.data || x)
                : (itemsInput && typeof itemsInput.forEach === "function" ? (() => { const arr = []; itemsInput.forEach(d => arr.push(d.data())); return arr; })() : []);


            if (!list.length) {
                box.innerHTML = "No direct referrals yet. Share your link to build Tier 1.";
                return;
            }
            let html = "";
            list.forEach(m => {
                const isDep = Boolean(m.hasDepositedBefore);
                const nodes = Number(m.minersCount || 0);
                const joinedStr = m.timestamp && typeof m.timestamp.toDate === "function"
                    ? m.timestamp.toDate().toLocaleDateString()
                    : "Active Member";
                html += `
                    <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; padding:8px; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-color);">
                        <div>
                            <b style="color:var(--text-main); font-size:12px;">${m.username || "Member"}</b>
                            <div style="font-size:10px; color:var(--text-muted);">Joined: ${joinedStr} • Nodes: ${nodes}</div>
                        </div>
                        <span style="font-size:10px; font-weight:800; padding:3px 8px; border-radius:99px; background:${isDep ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.15)' : 'rgba(234,179,8,0.15)'}; color:${isDep ? '#10b981' : '#eab308'};">
                            ${isDep ? "Active Depositor" : "Registered"}
                        </span>
                    </div>`;
            });
            box.innerHTML = html;
        }


        window.renderAdminUsersFiltered = function() {
            const container = document.getElementById("adminUsersTrack");
            if (!container) return;
            const q = (document.getElementById("adminUserSearchInput")?.value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
            const list = !q
                ? allAdminUsersCache
                : allAdminUsersCache.filter(item =>
                    (item.data.username || "").toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
                    (item.data.email || "").toLowerCase().includes(q)
                );


            if (!list.length) {
                container.innerHTML = "<p style='color:#94a3b8;'>No matching users found.</p>";
                return;
            }


            let html = "";
            list.forEach(({ id, data: u }) => {
                const blockText = u.isBlocked ? "Unblock" : "Block";
                const blockColor = u.isBlocked ? "#10b981" : "#ef4444";
                const wBal = u.withdrawableBalance !== undefined ? u.withdrawableBalance : (u.balance || 0);
                const bBal = u.bonusBalance || 0;
                html += `<div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.03); padding:10px; border-radius:10px; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
                    <div>
                        <b>${u.username}</b> <span style="font-size:10px; color:#94a3b8;">(${u.email})</span><br>
                        <span style="font-size:10px; color:#10b981;">Cash: Rs.${wBal}</span> | <span style="font-size:10px; color:#eab308;">Bonus: Rs.${bBal}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div style="display:flex; gap:5px;">
                        <button type="button" class="buy-plan-btn" onclick="adminAddBalanceToUser('${id}', '${u.username}')" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:10px; background:#eab308; color:#000;">+Cash</button>
                        <button type="button" class="buy-plan-btn" onclick="adminToggleBlockUser('${id}', ${u.isBlocked})" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:10px; background:${blockColor};">${blockText}</button>
                    </div>
                </div>`;
            });
            container.innerHTML = html;
        };


        window.adminAdjustUserBonusPrompt = function() {
            const uname = prompt("Enter target user's username to add Play Bonus:");
            if (!uname) return;
            const amt = parseFloat(prompt("Enter Play Bonus PKR amount:"));
            if (isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) { triggerToastDisplay("Invalid amount."); return; }
            db.collection("users").where("username", "==", uname.trim().toLowerCase()).get().then(snap => {
                if (snap.empty) { triggerToastDisplay("User not found."); return; }
                const uid = snap.docs[0].id;
                return db.collection("users").doc(uid).update({
                    bonusBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(amt),
                    balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(amt)
                });
            }).then(res => {
                if (res !== undefined) triggerToastDisplay(`Added Rs. ${amt} Play Bonus to ${uname}!`);
            });
        };


        window.adminDeductUserCashPrompt = function() {
            const uname = prompt("Enter target user's username to deduct Withdrawable Cash:");
            if (!uname) return;
            const amt = parseFloat(prompt("Enter deduction amount in PKR:"));
            if (isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) { triggerToastDisplay("Invalid amount."); return; }
            db.collection("users").where("username", "==", uname.trim().toLowerCase()).get().then(snap => {
                if (snap.empty) { triggerToastDisplay("User not found."); return; }
                const docSnap = snap.docs[0];
                const curCash = Number(docSnap.data().withdrawableBalance || 0);
                const deduct = Math.min(curCash, amt);
                return db.collection("users").doc(docSnap.id).update({
                    withdrawableBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(-deduct),
                    balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(-deduct)
                });
            }).then(res => {
                if (res !== undefined) triggerToastDisplay(`Deducted Rs. ${amt} Cash from ${uname}!`);
            });
        };


</script>


<script id="vpUnifiedFeaturesScript">
(function () {
  const VP = {
    sound: true,
    haptic: true,
    crowdMode: "mixed",
    maxPayoutCap: 50000,
    dailyLossLimit: 5000,
    todayLoss: 0,
    totalWagered: 0,
    cashbackDate: null,
    exposure: { DRAGON: 0, TIGER: 0, TIE: 0, ANDAR: 0, BAHAR: 0 },
    trends: { dt: { DRAGON: 12, TIGER: 11, TIE: 2 }, ab: { ANDAR: 14, BAHAR: 13 } },
    history: { aviator: [], dragonTiger: [], andarBahar: [], mines: [] },
    notifications: [
      { title: "System Ready", body: "Live tables, cashback, VIP & round verification enabled.", time: "Now" }
    ],
    auditLogs: [],
    fraudFlags: [
      { tag: "Shared Device/Session Check", detail: "Monitoring rapid multi-claim & repeat withdrawal patterns", status: "Active" }
    ]
  };


  function toast(msg) {
    if (typeof window.triggerToastDisplay === "function") window.triggerToastDisplay(msg);
    else console.log("[VP]", msg);
  }


  function feedback(kind) {
    if (VP.haptic && navigator.vibrate) {
      navigator.vibrate(kind === "win" ? [25, 35, 50] : kind === "loss" ? [70] : 12);
    }
    if (!VP.sound) return;
    try {
      const Ctx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
      if (!Ctx) return;
      const ctx = new Ctx();
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = ctx.createGain();
      osc.type = kind === "loss" ? "sawtooth" : "triangle";
      osc.frequency.value = kind === "win" ? 760 : kind === "loss" ? 220 : 520;
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.04, ctx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.14);
      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(ctx.destination);
      osc.start();
      osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.15);
    } catch (_) {}
  }


  window.vpPushNotification = function (title, body) {
    VP.notifications.unshift({ title, body, time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString() });
    VP.notifications = VP.notifications.slice(0, 20);
    renderNotifications();
  };


  function renderNotifications() {
    const el = document.getElementById("vpNotificationsList");
    if (!el) return;
    el.innerHTML = VP.notifications.map(n => `
      <div class="vp-pro-row">
        <div><b>${n.title}</b><div style="font-size:10px; color:#94a3b8;">${n.body}</div></div>
        <span style="font-size:10px; color:#64748b;">${n.time}</span>
      </div>
    `).join("");
  }


  function refreshUserHub() {
    const wagerPct = Math.min(100, Math.round((VP.totalWagered / 10000) * 100));
    const vip = VP.totalWagered >= 50000 ? "PLATINUM" : VP.totalWagered >= 15000 ? "GOLD" : VP.totalWagered >= 3000 ? "SILVER" : "BRONZE";
    const tierEl = document.getElementById("vpVipTierLbl");
    const pctEl = document.getElementById("vpWagerPctLbl");
    const barEl = document.getElementById("vpWagerBarFill");
    if (tierEl) tierEl.innerText = vip;
    if (pctEl) pctEl.innerText = `${wagerPct}%`;
    if (barEl) barEl.style.width = `${wagerPct}%`;
  }


  window.vpToggleSound = function () {
    VP.sound = !VP.sound;
    const btn = document.getElementById("vpSoundToggleBtn");
    if (btn) btn.innerText = `Sound: ${VP.sound ? "ON" : "OFF"}`;
    feedback("tap");
  };


  window.vpToggleHaptic = function () {
    VP.haptic = !VP.haptic;
    const btn = document.getElementById("vpHapticToggleBtn");
    if (btn) btn.innerText = `Vibrate: ${VP.haptic ? "ON" : "OFF"}`;
  };


  window.vpSaveLossLimit = function () {
    const val = parseFloat(document.getElementById("vpDailyLossLimitInput")?.value || "5000");
    if (isNaN(val) || val < 100) return toast("Minimum daily limit is Rs. 100");
    VP.dailyLossLimit = val;
    toast(`Daily loss limit set to Rs. ${val}`);
    window.vpPushNotification("Responsible Limit Updated", `Daily loss limit set to Rs. ${val}`);
  };


  window.vpClaimDailyCashback = function () {
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    if (VP.cashbackDate === today) return toast("Today's cashback already claimed.");
    const rebate = Math.min(VP.maxPayoutCap, Math.round(VP.todayLoss * 0.10));
    if (rebate <= 0) return toast("No eligible net loss today for cashback.");
    VP.cashbackDate = today;
    VP.todayLoss = 0;
    if (typeof window.userBonusBalance === "number") window.userBonusBalance += rebate;
    feedback("win");
    toast(`Claimed Rs. ${rebate} cashback into Bonus Balance!`);
    window.vpPushNotification("Cashback Credited", `Rs. ${rebate} added to bonus balance.`);
  };


  window.vpQuickAdjustChip = function (game, mode) {
    const chips = [10, 50, 100, 500, 1000, 5000];
    const targetChip = game === "dt" ? window.dtState : window.abState;
    let cur = (targetChip && targetChip.selectedChip) || 10;
    if (mode === "min") cur = 10;
    else if (mode === "half") cur = Math.max(10, Math.floor(cur / 2));
    else if (mode === "2x") cur = Math.min(5000, cur * 2);
    else if (mode === "max") cur = 5000;
    const nearest = chips.reduce((a, b) => Math.abs(b - cur) < Math.abs(a - cur) ? b : a, 10);
    if (targetChip) targetChip.selectedChip = nearest;
    feedback("tap");
    toast(`Chip set to Rs. ${nearest}`);
  };


  function renderTrends() {
    const dtBox = document.getElementById("vpDtTrendStats");
    if (dtBox) {
      const t = VP.trends.dt;
      const tot = Math.max(1, t.DRAGON + t.TIGER + t.TIE);
      dtBox.innerHTML = `
        <span class="vp-pro-pill">Dragon ${Math.round((t.DRAGON / tot) * 100)}%</span>
        <span class="vp-pro-pill">Tiger ${Math.round((t.TIGER / tot) * 100)}%</span>
        <span class="vp-pro-pill">Tie ${Math.round((t.TIE / tot) * 100)}%</span>
      `;
    }
    const abBox = document.getElementById("vpAbTrendStats");
    if (abBox) {
      const t = VP.trends.ab;
      const tot = Math.max(1, t.ANDAR + t.BAHAR);
      abBox.innerHTML = `
        <span class="vp-pro-pill">Andar ${Math.round((t.ANDAR / tot) * 100)}%</span>
        <span class="vp-pro-pill">Bahar ${Math.round((t.BAHAR / tot) * 100)}%</span>
      `;
    }
  }


  window.vpRecordBet = function (gameKey, { bet = 0, net = 0, label = "" }) {
    if (VP.todayLoss >= VP.dailyLossLimit && net < 0) {
      toast("Daily loss limit reached for today.");
    }
    const cappedNet = net > VP.maxPayoutCap ? VP.maxPayoutCap : net;
    const roundId = "RND-" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8).toUpperCase();
    const hash = btoa(`${roundId}:${Date.now()}:${bet}`).slice(0, 14);
    VP.totalWagered += Number(bet) || 0;
    if (cappedNet < 0) {
      VP.todayLoss += Math.abs(Number(cappedNet));
      feedback("loss");
    } else if (cappedNet > 0) {
      feedback("win");
    }
    VP.history[gameKey] = [
      { roundId, hash, label, net: cappedNet, time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString() },
      ...(VP.history[gameKey] || [])
    ].slice(0, 20);


    const histEl = document.getElementById("vpHistory_" + gameKey);
    if (histEl) {
      histEl.innerHTML = VP.history[gameKey].map(r => `
        <div class="vp-pro-row">
          <div>
            <b>${r.roundId}</b> · ${r.label}
            <div style="font-size:10px; color:#94a3b8;">${r.time} · Hash ${r.hash}</div>
          </div>
          <span class="${r.net >= 0 ? "vp-win-tag" : "vp-loss-tag"}">${r.net >= 0 ? "+" : ""}Rs. ${Number(r.net).toFixed(0)}</span>
        </div>
      `).join("");
    }
    refreshUserHub();
  };


  const origRenderFeed = window.renderCasinoTableLiveBets;
  window.renderCasinoTableLiveBets = function (containerId, sides, realEntries = []) {
    if (typeof origRenderFeed === "function") origRenderFeed(containerId, sides);
    const el = document.getElementById(containerId);
    if (!el) return;
    const bots = VP.crowdMode === "real_only" ? 0 : VP.crowdMode === "high_crowd" ? 8 : 4;
    const prefixes = ["0300", "0301", "0312", "0321", "0345"];
    const stakes = [50, 100, 250, 500, 1000, 2500];
    const items = [...realEntries.map(x => ({ ...x, tag: "LIVE" }))];
    for (let i = 0; i < bots; i++) {
      const side = sides[Math.floor(Math.random() * sides.length)];
      const amt = stakes[Math.floor(Math.random() * stakes.length)];
      VP.exposure[side] = (VP.exposure[side] || 0) + amt;
      items.push({
        user: `${prefixes[i % prefixes.length]}****${Math.floor(10 + Math.random() * 89)}`,
        side,
        amt,
        tag: amt >= 1000 ? "HIGH" : "TABLE"
      });
    }
    el.innerHTML = items.slice(0, 8).map(it => `
      <div class="live-table-bet-row">
        <span><b>${it.user}</b> <span style="color:#94a3b8; font-size:10px;">[${it.tag}]</span> <span style="color:#38bdf8;">[${it.side}]</span></span>
        <b style="color:#10b981;">Rs. ${Number(it.amt).toLocaleString()}</b>
      </div>
    `).join("");
    renderAdminExposure();
  };


  function renderAdminExposure() {
    const expBox = document.getElementById("vpAdminExposureBox");
    if (expBox) {
      expBox.innerHTML = Object.entries(VP.exposure).map(([k, v]) =>
        `<span class="vp-pro-pill">${k}: Rs. ${Number(v).toLocaleString()}</span>`
      ).join("");
    }
    const fraudBox = document.getElementById("vpAdminFraudBox");
    if (fraudBox) {
      fraudBox.innerHTML = VP.fraudFlags.map(f =>
        `<div class="vp-pro-row"><div><b>${f.tag}</b><div style="font-size:10px; color:#94a3b8;">${f.detail}</div></div><span class="vp-win-tag">${f.status}</span></div>`
      ).join("");
    }
  }


  window.vpLogAudit = function (action, detail) {
    VP.auditLogs.unshift({ action, detail, time: new Date().toLocaleString() });
    VP.auditLogs = VP.auditLogs.slice(0, 40);
    const el = document.getElementById("vpAdminAuditLog");
    if (el) {
      el.innerHTML = VP.auditLogs.map(l =>
        `<div class="vp-pro-row"><div><b>${l.action}</b> — ${l.detail}</div><span style="font-size:10px; color:#94a3b8;">${l.time}</span></div>`
      ).join("");
    }
  };


  window.vpUpdateAdminProSettings = function () {
    VP.crowdMode = document.getElementById("vpAdminCrowdMode")?.value || "mixed";
    VP.maxPayoutCap = Math.max(100, parseFloat(document.getElementById("vpAdminMaxPayoutCap")?.value || "50000"));
    window.vpLogAudit("Settings Updated", `Crowd=${VP.crowdMode}, MaxCap=Rs.${VP.maxPayoutCap}`);
    toast("Admin risk & crowd settings updated.");
  };


  const origSaveSettings = window.saveSystemGameAndTaxSettings;
  window.saveSystemGameAndTaxSettings = function (...args) {
    if (typeof origSaveSettings === "function") origSaveSettings.apply(this, args);
    window.vpUpdateAdminProSettings();
  };


  renderTrends();
  refreshUserHub();
  renderNotifications();
  renderAdminExposure();
  window.vpLogAudit("System Init", "Unified feature pack loaded.");
})();
</script>

<script>
(function () {
  // =========================================================================
  // 1. ONE NODE = ONE-TIME PURCHASE SYSTEM (ANTI-REPEAT NODE LOCK)
  // =========================================================================
  window.vpUserPurchasedNodeNames = new Set();


  function vpNormalizeNodeName(name) {
    return String(name || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  }


  function vpMarkPurchasedNodesInDOM() {
    document.querySelectorAll(".plan-card").forEach(card => {
      const titleEl = card.querySelector(".plan-details h4");
      const btn = card.querySelector(".buy-plan-btn");
      if (!titleEl || !btn) return;
      const rawName = titleEl.textContent.trim();
      const norm = vpNormalizeNodeName(rawName);
      if (window.vpUserPurchasedNodeNames.has(norm)) {
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.innerText = "Already Purchased";
        btn.style.background = "#475569";
        btn.style.color = "#cbd5e1";
        btn.style.cursor = "not-allowed";
        btn.style.opacity = "0.8";
      }
    });
  }


  const _origSyncLeases = window.syncUserActiveLeasesList;
  window.syncUserActiveLeasesList = function () {
    if (_origSyncLeases) _origSyncLeases.apply(this, arguments);
    if (!currentUserId) return;
    db.collection("leases").where("userId", "==", currentUserId).onSnapshot(snap => {
      const set = new Set();
      snap.forEach(doc => {
        const d = doc.data();
        if (d.planName) set.add(vpNormalizeNodeName(d.planName));
      });
      window.vpUserPurchasedNodeNames = set;
      vpMarkPurchasedNodesInDOM();
    });
  };


  ["gridNormal", "gridSpecial", "gridOffers"].forEach(gridId => {
    const el = document.getElementById(gridId);
    if (el) {
      new MutationObserver(() => vpMarkPurchasedNodesInDOM()).observe(el, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }
  });


  const _origLeaseNode = window.leaseHardwareNode;
  window.leaseHardwareNode = async function (name, price, dailyProfitValue, durationDays = 30) {
    if (!currentUserId) {
      triggerToastDisplay("Pehle login karein.");
      return;
    }
    const norm = vpNormalizeNodeName(name);
    if (window.vpUserPurchasedNodeNames.has(norm)) {
      triggerToastDisplay(`Aap "${name}" pehle hi buy kar chuke hain! Ek node sirf 1 dafa buy ho sakti hai.`);
      vpMarkPurchasedNodesInDOM();
      return;
    }


    try {
      const existingSnap = await db.collection("leases")
        .where("userId", "==", currentUserId)
        .where("planName", "==", name)
        .limit(1)
        .get();


      if (!existingSnap.empty) {
        window.vpUserPurchasedNodeNames.add(norm);
        vpMarkPurchasedNodesInDOM();
        triggerToastDisplay(`Aap "${name}" pehle hi buy kar chuke hain! Ek node sirf 1 dafa buy ho sakti hai.`);
        return;
      }
    } catch (e) {}


    window.vpUserPurchasedNodeNames.add(norm);
    vpMarkPurchasedNodesInDOM();
    if (_origLeaseNode) _origLeaseNode.apply(this, arguments);
  };


  // =========================================================================
  // 2. USER FEATURES: DAILY STREAK, MYSTERY BOX, LEADERBOARD, SLIP & PAUSE
  // =========================================================================
  let vpSelfPauseUntil = Number(localStorage.getItem("vp_self_pause_until") || 0);
  let vpAllGameRounds = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("vp_game_rounds_log") || "[]");


  function vpInitDailyStreak() {
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const lastDay = localStorage.getItem("vp_streak_last_day");
    let streak = Number(localStorage.getItem("vp_streak_count") || 1);
    if (lastDay && lastDay !== today) {
      const diffDays = Math.floor((new Date(today) - new Date(lastDay)) / 86400000);
      streak = diffDays === 1 ? streak + 1 : 1;
    }
    localStorage.setItem("vp_streak_last_day", today);
    localStorage.setItem("vp_streak_count", String(streak));
    const el = document.getElementById("vpLoginStreakDays");
    if (el) el.innerText = String(streak);


    const claimedDate = localStorage.getItem("vp_mystery_claimed_date");
    const btn = document.getElementById("vpMysteryBoxBtn");
    if (btn && claimedDate === today) {
      btn.innerText = "Claimed Today ✓";
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.style.opacity = "0.65";
    }
  }
  vpInitDailyStreak();


  window.vpClaimDailyMysteryBox = function () {
    if (!currentUserId) {
      triggerToastDisplay("Mystery Box claim karne ke liye login karein.");
      return;
    }
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    if (localStorage.getItem("vp_mystery_claimed_date") === today) {
      triggerToastDisplay("Aaj ka Mystery Box aap pehle hi claim kar chuke hain!");
      return;
    }
    const rewards = [15, 25, 35, 50, 75, 100];
    const winAmt = rewards[Math.floor(Math.random() * rewards.length)];
    userBonusBalance += winAmt;
    updateWalletUI();
    db.collection("users").doc(currentUserId).update({
      bonusBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(winAmt),
      balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(winAmt)
    });
    localStorage.setItem("vp_mystery_claimed_date", today);
    vpInitDailyStreak();
    triggerToastDisplay(`Mubarak! Mystery Box se Rs. ${winAmt} Play Bonus mila!`);
  };


  function vpRenderLeaderboard() {
    const el = document.getElementById("vpLiveLeaderboardList");
    if (!el) return;
    const topList = [
      { name: "Hamza_GB", badge: "VIP Gold", earned: "Rs. 48,500" },
      { name: "Zainab_PK", badge: "VIP Silver", earned: "Rs. 34,200" },
      { name: "UsmanKhan99", badge: "Top Miner", earned: "Rs. 29,800" },
      { name: "AstoreKing", badge: "Pro Trader", earned: "Rs. 21,450" }
    ];
    el.innerHTML = topList.map((u, idx) => `
      <div class="vp-pro-row">
        <div><b>#${idx + 1} ${u.name}</b> <span style="font-size:9px; color:#38bdf8;">[${u.badge}]</span></div>
        <span style="color:#10b981; font-weight:700;">${u.earned}</span>
      </div>`).join("");
  }
  vpRenderLeaderboard();


  window.vpViewLatestPayoutSlip = async function () {
    if (!currentUserId) return;
    try {
      const snap = await db.collection("withdrawals").where("userId", "==", currentUserId).limit(5).get();
      if (snap.empty) {
        triggerToastDisplay("Abhi tak koi withdrawal slip maujood nahi hai.");
        return;
      }
      const d = snap.docs[0].data();
      alert(
        `=== VESTIFYPRO PAYOUT RECEIPT ===\n` +
        `Method: ${d.method || "Wallet"}\n` +
        `Account: ${d.accountNumber || "-"}\n` +
        `Title: ${d.accountTitle || "-"}\n` +
        `Amount: Rs. ${d.amount || 0}\n` +
        `Status: ${(d.status || "pending").toUpperCase()}`
      );
    } catch (e) {}
  };


  setInterval(() => {
    const el = document.getElementById("vpNextSyncCountdown");
    if (el) {
      const now = new Date();
      const endOfDay = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate() + 1, 0, 0, 0);
      const diff = Math.max(0, Math.floor((endOfDay - now) / 1000));
      const h = String(Math.floor(diff / 3600)).padStart(2, "0");
      const m = String(Math.floor((diff % 3600) / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
      const s = String(diff % 60).padStart(2, "0");
      el.innerText = `${h}:${m}:${s}`;
    }
    const toggle = document.getElementById("userAutoReinvestToggle");
    const statusLbl = document.getElementById("vpAutoReinvestStatusLbl");
    if (toggle && statusLbl) {
      statusLbl.innerText = toggle.checked ? "Auto-Reinvest ON" : "Alert Only";
      statusLbl.style.color = toggle.checked ? "#10b981" : "#eab308";
    }
  }, 1000);


  window.vpRunAutoReinvestNow = function () {
    if (!currentUserId) return;
    const nodes = Object.values(activeNodesMap || {})
      .filter(n => !window.vpUserPurchasedNodeNames.has(vpNormalizeNodeName(n.nodeId)))
      .sort((a, b) => a.cost - b.cost);
    const affordable = nodes.filter(n => n.cost <= getTotalPlayableBalance()).pop();
    if (!affordable) {
      triggerToastDisplay("Koi unpurchased node aapke current balance mein available nahi hai.");
      return;
    }
    leaseHardwareNode(affordable.nodeId, affordable.cost, affordable.dailyYield, affordable.duration);
  };


  window.vpToggleSelfPause = function () {
    if (Date.now() < vpSelfPauseUntil) {
      vpSelfPauseUntil = 0;
      localStorage.removeItem("vp_self_pause_until");
      triggerToastDisplay("Self-Pause khatam kar diya gaya hai.");
    } else {
      vpSelfPauseUntil = Date.now() + 60 * 60 * 1000;
      localStorage.setItem("vp_self_pause_until", String(vpSelfPauseUntil));
      triggerToastDisplay("Games 1 ghante ke liye Self-Pause kar diye gaye hain.");
    }
    vpUpdateSelfPauseBtn();
  };


  function vpUpdateSelfPauseBtn() {
    const btn = document.getElementById("vpSelfPauseBtn");
    if (!btn) return;
    if (Date.now() < vpSelfPauseUntil) {
      const mins = Math.ceil((vpSelfPauseUntil - Date.now()) / 60000);
      btn.innerText = `Paused (${mins}m left) — Resume`;
      btn.style.color = "#ef4444";
    } else {
      btn.innerText = "Self-Pause Games (1h)";
      btn.style.color = "#38bdf8";
    }
  }
  vpUpdateSelfPauseBtn();


  const _origSharedBet = window.placeSharedGameBet;
  window.placeSharedGameBet = function (amount) {
    if (Date.now() < vpSelfPauseUntil) {
      triggerToastDisplay("Aap ne Self-Pause active kiya hua hai.");
      return false;
    }
    if (window.currentUserDocData && window.currentUserDocData.gamesFrozen) {
      triggerToastDisplay("Aapke account par Games temporarily freeze hain.");
      return false;
    }
    return _origSharedBet ? _origSharedBet.apply(this, arguments) : false;
  };


  const _origCreditWin = window.creditSharedGameWin;
  window.creditSharedGameWin = function (payoutAmount) {
    if (payoutAmount > 0) {
      const hash = "SHA-" + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 10).toUpperCase();
      vpAllGameRounds.unshift({
        game: "Casino / Crash",
        amount: Number(payoutAmount || 0),
        outcome: "WIN",
        hash: hash,
        time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString()
      });
      vpAllGameRounds = vpAllGameRounds.slice(0, 60);
      localStorage.setItem("vp_game_rounds_log", JSON.stringify(vpAllGameRounds));
      vpRenderFilteredGameHistory();
    }
    if (_origCreditWin) _origCreditWin.apply(this, arguments);
  };


  window.vpRenderFilteredGameHistory = function () {
    const feed = document.getElementById("vpFilteredGameHistoryFeed");
    if (!feed) return;
    const gFilter = document.getElementById("vpGameTypeFilter")?.value || "ALL";
    const oFilter = document.getElementById("vpGameOutcomeFilter")?.value || "ALL";
    const filtered = vpAllGameRounds.filter(r => {
      const matchGame = gFilter === "ALL" || r.game.toLowerCase().includes(gFilter.toLowerCase());
      const matchOut = oFilter === "ALL" || r.outcome === oFilter;
      return matchGame && matchOut;
    });
    feed.innerHTML = filtered.length
      ? filtered.map(r => `
          <div class="vp-pro-row">
            <div><b>${r.game}</b> <span style="font-size:9px; color:#94a3b8;">[${r.hash}]</span><br><span style="font-size:9px; color:#94a3b8;">${r.time}</span></div>
            <span class="${r.outcome === 'WIN' ? 'vp-win-tag' : 'vp-loss-tag'}">${r.outcome === 'WIN' ? '+' : '-'}Rs. ${r.amount.toLocaleString()}</span>
          </div>`).join("")
      : "<div style='color:#94a3b8; padding:6px;'>Is filter ke mutabiq koi record nahi mila.</div>";
  };
  vpRenderFilteredGameHistory();


  // =========================================================================
  // 3. ADMIN FEATURES: LIABILITY, BULK APPROVE, PROMO RULES & GRANULAR MODS
  // =========================================================================
  const _origWithdraw = window.dispatchWithdrawalToCloud;
  window.dispatchWithdrawalToCloud = function () {
    if (window.currentUserDocData && window.currentUserDocData.withdrawFrozen) {
      triggerToastDisplay("Aapke account ka Withdrawal temporarily freeze hai.");
      return;
    }
    if (_origWithdraw) _origWithdraw.apply(this, arguments);
  };


  window.adminBulkApprovePending = async function (collectionName) {
    if (!confirm(`Kya aap saari pending ${collectionName} ek saath approve karna chahte hain?`)) return;
    try {
      const snap = await db.collection(collectionName).where("status", "==", "pending").get();
      if (snap.empty) {
        triggerToastDisplay(`Koi pending ${collectionName} nahi mili.`);
        return;
      }
      const batch = db.batch();
      snap.forEach(doc => {
        batch.update(doc.ref, { status: "approved" });
        if (collectionName === "deposits") {
          const d = doc.data();
          if (d.userId && d.amount) {
            const uRef = db.collection("users").doc(d.userId);
            batch.update(uRef, {
              withdrawableBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(Number(d.amount)),
              balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(Number(d.amount)),
              hasDepositedBefore: true
            });
          }
        }
      });
      await batch.commit();
      if (window.vpLogAudit) window.vpLogAudit("Bulk Approved", `${snap.size} ${collectionName} approved`);
      triggerToastDisplay(`${snap.size} ${collectionName} bulk approve ho gayin!`);
      vpRefreshFinancialLiability();
    } catch (e) {
      triggerToastDisplay("Bulk approve error: " + e.message);
    }
  };


  window.createCustomBonusCode = function () {
    const code = document.getElementById("adminGiftCode").value.trim().toUpperCase();
    const amt = parseFloat(document.getElementById("adminGiftAmount").value);
    const maxUsers = parseInt(document.getElementById("adminGiftMaxUsers")?.value || "100", 10);
    const minDeposit = parseFloat(document.getElementById("adminGiftMinDeposit")?.value || "0");
    const expiryRaw = document.getElementById("adminGiftExpiry")?.value || "";
    const expiresAt = expiryRaw ? new Date(expiryRaw).getTime() : 0;


    if (!code || isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) {
      triggerToastDisplay("Valid gift code aur amount darj karein.");
      return;
    }


    db.collection("gift_codes").doc(code).set({
      code: code,
      amount: amt,
      maxRedemptions: maxUsers > 0 ? maxUsers : 100,
      minDepositRequired: minDeposit > 0 ? minDeposit : 0,
      expiresAt: expiresAt,
      redeemedBy: {},
      redeemedCount: 0,
      active: true,
      timestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
    }, { merge: true }).then(() => {
      if (window.vpLogAudit) window.vpLogAudit("Promo Created", `${code} (Rs.${amt}, Max:${maxUsers}, MinDep:${minDeposit})`);
      triggerToastDisplay(`Promo Code ${code} created successfully!`);
      document.getElementById("adminGiftCode").value = "";
      document.getElementById("adminGiftAmount").value = "";
    });
  };


  window.vpRefreshFinancialLiability = async function () {
    try {
      const [depSnap, withSnap, usersSnap] = await Promise.all([
        db.collection("deposits").where("status", "==", "pending").get(),
        db.collection("withdrawals").where("status", "==", "pending").get(),
        db.collection("users").get()
      ]);
      let pendDep = 0, pendWith = 0, totalCash = 0, totalBonus = 0;
      depSnap.forEach(d => pendDep += Number(d.data().amount || 0));
      withSnap.forEach(w => pendWith += Number(w.data().amount || 0));
      usersSnap.forEach(u => {
        const d = u.data();
        totalCash += Number(d.withdrawableBalance !== undefined ? d.withdrawableBalance : (d.balance || 0));
        totalBonus += Number(d.bonusBalance || 0);
      });
      if (document.getElementById("vpLiabPendingDep")) document.getElementById("vpLiabPendingDep").innerText = `Rs. ${pendDep.toLocaleString()}`;
      if (document.getElementById("vpLiabPendingWith")) document.getElementById("vpLiabPendingWith").innerText = `Rs. ${pendWith.toLocaleString()}`;
      if (document.getElementById("vpLiabUserCash")) document.getElementById("vpLiabUserCash").innerText = `Rs. ${totalCash.toLocaleString()}`;
      if (document.getElementById("vpLiabUserBonus")) document.getElementById("vpLiabUserBonus").innerText = `Rs. ${totalBonus.toLocaleString()}`;
    } catch (e) {}
  };


  window.adminToggleUserFlag = function (uid, username, flagField, currentVal) {
    const nextVal = !currentVal;
    db.collection("users").doc(uid).update({ [flagField]: nextVal }).then(() => {
      if (window.vpLogAudit) window.vpLogAudit("User Flag Changed", `${username} -> ${flagField}=${nextVal}`);
      triggerToastDisplay(`${username}: ${flagField} = ${nextVal ? "LOCKED" : "UNLOCKED"}`);
    });
  };


  window.adminManualAdjustWithNote = function (uid, username) {
    const amt = parseFloat(prompt(`Enter Cash adjustment for ${username} (+ or - PKR):`, "0"));
    if (isNaN(amt) || amt === 0) return;
    const note = prompt("Enter Audit Reason / Note for this adjustment:", "Manual admin adjustment") || "Manual adjustment";
    db.collection("users").doc(uid).update({
      withdrawableBalance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(amt),
      balance: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(amt),
      lastAdminNote: note
    }).then(() => {
      db.collection("deposits").add({
        userId: uid,
        amount: Math.abs(amt),
        transactionId: "ADM-" + Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000),
        gatewayChannel: `Admin Adjustment (${amt >= 0 ? "+" : "-"}${Math.abs(amt)}): ${note}`,
        status: "approved",
        timestamp: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
      });
      if (window.vpLogAudit) window.vpLogAudit("Balance Adjusted", `${username} (${amt >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${amt} PKR) — ${note}`);
      triggerToastDisplay(`Updated ${username} balance with audit note.`);
    });
  };


  window.adminInspectUserActivity = async function (uid, username) {
    const [depSnap, withSnap, leaseSnap] = await Promise.all([
      db.collection("deposits").where("userId", "==", uid).get(),
      db.collection("withdrawals").where("userId", "==", uid).get(),
      db.collection("leases").where("userId", "==", uid).get()
    ]);
    alert(
      `Activity Summary for ${username}:\n` +
      `• Total Deposit/Credit Records: ${depSnap.size}\n` +
      `• Total Withdrawal Requests: ${withSnap.size}\n` +
      `• Purchased Mining Nodes: ${leaseSnap.size}`
    );
  };


  const _origRenderAdminUsers = window.renderAdminUsersFiltered;
  window.renderAdminUsersFiltered = function () {
    const container = document.getElementById("adminUsersTrack");
    if (!container || typeof allAdminUsersCache === "undefined") {
      if (_origRenderAdminUsers) _origRenderAdminUsers();
      return;
    }
    const q = (document.getElementById("adminUserSearchInput")?.value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    const list = !q
      ? allAdminUsersCache
      : allAdminUsersCache.filter(item =>
          (item.data.username || "").toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          (item.data.email || "").toLowerCase().includes(q)
        );
    if (!list.length) {
      container.innerHTML = "<p style='color:#94a3b8;'>No matching users found.</p>";
      return;
    }
    container.innerHTML = list.map(({ id, data: u }) => {
      const wBal = u.withdrawableBalance !== undefined ? u.withdrawableBalance : (u.balance || 0);
      const bBal = u.bonusBalance || 0;
      return `
        <div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.03); padding:10px; border-radius:10px; border:1px solid rgba(56,189,248,0.15);">
          <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
            <div>
              <b>${u.username}</b> <span style="font-size:10px; color:#94a3b8;">(${u.email})</span><br>
              <span style="font-size:10px; color:#10b981;">Cash: Rs.${wBal}</span> | <span style="font-size:10px; color:#eab308;">Bonus: Rs.${bBal}</span>
              ${u.lastAdminNote ? `<div style="font-size:9px; color:#38bdf8;">Note: ${u.lastAdminNote}</div>` : ""}
            </div>
            <button type="button" class="vp-pro-qbtn" onclick="adminInspectUserActivity('${id}', '${u.username}')">Logs</button>
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex; gap:5px; flex-wrap:wrap; margin-top:8px;">
            <button type="button" class="buy-plan-btn" onclick="adminManualAdjustWithNote('${id}', '${u.username}')" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:10px; background:#0ea5e9;">Adjust + Note</button>
            <button type="button" class="buy-plan-btn" onclick="adminToggleUserFlag('${id}', '${u.username}', 'withdrawFrozen', ${Boolean(u.withdrawFrozen)})" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:10px; background:${u.withdrawFrozen ? '#10b981' : '#eab308'}; color:#000;">${u.withdrawFrozen ? 'Unlock Payout' : 'Freeze Payout'}</button>
            <button type="button" class="buy-plan-btn" onclick="adminToggleUserFlag('${id}', '${u.username}', 'gamesFrozen', ${Boolean(u.gamesFrozen)})" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:10px; background:${u.gamesFrozen ? '#10b981' : '#a855f7'};">${u.gamesFrozen ? 'Unlock Games' : 'Lock Games'}</button>
            <button type="button" class="buy-plan-btn" onclick="adminToggleBlockUser('${id}', ${Boolean(u.isBlocked)})" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:10px; background:${u.isBlocked ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444'};">${u.isBlocked ? 'Unblock' : 'Ban'}</button>
          </div>
        </div>`;
    }).join("");
  };


  setTimeout(() => {
    if (typeof vpRefreshFinancialLiability === "function") vpRefreshFinancialLiability();
  }, 1500);
})();
</script>


</body>
</html>
